Mirror for protected highs (K18: the high above a swing low that was closed through with aggression). (5) Outcomes: break rate of protected lows by 16:00; MFE (points) from each new protected low to the next break; time-to-break.
- **Constants.** Printed: none numeric. Source-unspecified: fractal length, q75, 5-bar quiet window.

Prior swing high 102.0; close 102.25 at bar 17 (escape); bars 18–22 min low 100.75 (> 100.5 = 100 + 2 ticks) ✓ → `protected_low = 100.0` at bar 17. Break: 1m close 99.75 at bar 41 → `broken = 1`, MFE = max high (104.5) − 100.0 = 4.5 pts.

(5) Retest: later touch `t2` of the zone; outcome = G-default reject in the imbalance direction (R = candle range, k = 15). (6) Both-sides-absorbed flag: wick prints on both sides within 5 min → wait for `b.c1` out of the range.

### R-F17 — Refill-zone touch · pass 0.0062 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[REF p.5]`: "When a burst of large aggressive market orders trades at one price area (sixty, eighty, a hundred contracts hitting in seconds) that area stops being a random price … We call the cluster of prints a zone. The next time price returns to the zone, exactly one question matters: are the defenders still there?" `[REF p.8]`: "Only 42% of touches hold." `[REF p.10]`: "the median eventual winner first dips 18 ticks past the touch before it works." `[REF p.12]`: "rest a limit order 12 ticks inside the level, stop 32 ticks, target 96 ticks, cancel after 30 minutes." `[REF p.8]` families: "Memory: held earlier this session; prior-day defence. Construction: number & size of aggressive prints; zone width. Location: value-area edges, session extremes, distance from open. Flow & state: delta into the touch, approach speed, balance vs imbalance." `[REF p.23]`: "Hold rate, worst → best decile 25% → 63%." `[REF p.5]` figures: the zone is a shaded band with thickness built by circled prints, and the "textbook example" is MNQ (10 Jan 2025, 09:36–09:44 ET) captioned "bubble size = contracts in the aggressive print (≥40 shown)" with prints of 122–251 contracts; `[REF p.23]` data line: "NQ & MNQ, Dec 2024–Nov 2025"; `[REF p.17–18]`: the 100-configuration grid runs stops 25–65 ticks and targets 20–100 ticks with entry depth as the third axis; `[REF p.7]`: on the NQ chart the zones are rectangles several points tall.
- **Procedure.** (1) Zone, two printed readings: (a) per print — ≥ 3 same-side aggressive prints each ≥ 60 lots ("sixty, eighty, a hundred"; 60 default, 80/100 named) within 30 s (named); (b) per burst — same-side aggressive prints whose total ≥ 60 lots within the same window ("hitting in seconds"); bounds = min/max trade price; `known_at` = last print. The contract unit is unstated: the paper pools NQ and MNQ and its only sized figure is MNQ with prints ≥ 40 shown, while on the NQ MBP-1 tape the print-size q99 is 7 lots, so reading (a) on NQ is expected to be near-empty and both readings are printed with the unit named. (2) Touch: price returns into the zone after leaving it by ≥ 4 ticks (named). (3) Hold = penetration beyond the zone's far edge ≤ 32 ticks and no `b.c1` beyond it within 30 min, then price leaves on the defended side by ≥ 12 ticks (the deployed geometry as outcome definitions, not fills); break = penetration > 32 ticks. (4) Features frozen at the touch: memory (held earlier this session; defended prior day), construction (print count, total size, width), location (distance to prior VA edges / session extremes / open), flow (delta into the touch, approach speed). (5) Score: hold rate (42% base), by memory decile; penetration-depth distribution (18-tick median claim); no execution rows.
- **Constants.** Printed: 60/80/100 lots (NQ-or-MNQ unit unstated); ≥ 40 shown on the MNQ figure; 12 / 32 / 96 ticks; the grid bounds 25–65 (stop) and 20–100 (target) ticks; 30 min cancel; 42%; 18 ticks; 25%→63%. Source-unspecified: the 30-second cluster window, the leave distance, the per-print vs per-burst reading.
- **Different object if.** The cluster is ≥ 100 lots within 2 min at 2 ticks with a later return (that is `flow.refill.ontouch`, the retained function), or the outcome is a P&L bracket, or the lots are silently read as NQ contracts.
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Print threshold 60 | 80 | 100, per print | per burst; penetration tolerance 12 | 18 | 32 ticks and the printed grid bounds 25 | 65; cancel 15 | 30 | 60 min (RULES C3).
- **Fixture.** Prints: 65, 80, 70 lots (sells) at 100.25, 100.00, 100.25 within 12 s → `zone = [100.00, 100.25]` (width 1 tick). Price leaves to 101.5 (5 ticks ✓). Touch at 10:40 low 100.25; deepest dip 99.50 (2 ticks past the far edge 100.00 ≤ 32 ✓); no 1m close < 100.00 in 30 min; high 103.25 by 11:05 (≥ 12 ticks = 3.0 above 100.25 ✓) → `hold = 1`, `penetration = 2 ticks`.

### R-F18 — Squeeze without failure · pass 0.7264 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[CONT p.11]`: "this one is actually a squeeze, not an origin of the move, because it never failed the way the second trade's first attempt did. Sellers showed willingness to price lower and the move happened fast, with real aggression, no retest, no false start. Once buyers hit that move and got absorbed, the entry is available with a tight stop right there." `[OFM p.5]`: "A real squeeze is violent and quick … Watch the speed of tape when it happens." `[OFM p.14]`: "The squeeze fails with no aggressive orders at the failure at all: the speed of tape just dies" (the passive variant).
- **Procedure.** (1) Catalyst as in R-F15 step 1. (2) Release = 1m close beyond the extreme with `flow.tape.speed` (prints per second over 30 s) ≥ q90 of the session so far (named) — the fast release; every DeepCharts screenshot labels the panel "Speed of Tape (10)" and the unit of the 10 (prints or seconds) is not stated, so a 10-unit window is a named variant beside 30 s. (3) No failure = no `b.c1` back through the catalyst box within 15 min of the release (named). (4) Trigger = `flow.absorption.A` against the move at the first pullback (opposite aggression absorbed: advance against the squeeze ≤ 2 ticks). (5) Outcome: continuation = touch `t2` of the next level in the squeeze direction (prior H/L or `lvl.owed.nearest`) by 16:00; MFE in points from the trigger.
- **Constants.** Printed: none numeric. Source-unspecified: q90 tape speed, 15-min no-failure window.
- **Different object if.** The squeeze failed first (then it is R-F15), or the release is slow (passive variant, named).
- **Fixture.** Catalyst [89.75, 90.0] (sell wick prints at the low). Release close 89.25 at 10:12 with 30-s print rate 14/s vs q90 11/s ✓. No 1m close > 90.0 by 10:27 ✓ → `no_failure = 1`. Pullback: high 89.9 at 10:33, 2-min buy volume 3,650 ≥ q90 ✓, advance ≤ 2 ticks ✓ → `trigger = 1`; next level prior RTH L 84.0 touched 11:10 → `continuation = 1`.
- **Code.** **missing (input)** (no tape-speed object at the release, no catalyst). The 2026-09-10 rescoring sets `family_tape.py` `f18_squeeze` = a stacked 4× footprint without an on-touch refill (shared with `f15_ofm`); `formulas_flow.py::tape_speed_pps` (TAPE_WINDOW_S = 30) and `r_f18_squeeze` exist unwired.

### R-R01 — GEX regime gate · gap

- **Source.** `[GEX p.6]`: "GEX measures the dollar value of futures dealers must buy or sell to stay delta-neutral for a given move in price. The sign of that number is everything. LONG GAMMA: Positive GEX, stabilising … SHORT GAMMA: Negative GEX, amplifying." `[GEX p.7]`: "the level where net dealer gamma crosses from positive to negative. It is called the zero gamma level, or the gamma flip"; its figure is a GEXRADAR bar chart of net GEX by strike — red (negative) bars at the low strikes, green (positive) above, "P: Max Pain" marked at the far left, "GFlip" at the red-to-green boundary, "Call Wall ▲▲▲" at the tallest green bar and a "Vol Trig" line beside it. `[GEX p.13]`: "CALL WALL: Largest call gamma above. PUT WALL: Largest put gamma below. MAX PAIN: Strike where option value is lowest"; its chart draws three ranked call walls — ▲ 740.00, ▲▲ 732.00, ▲▲▲ 730.00 — around spot 731.98 (the third below spot) and "G-Flip 726.17" below, under the caption "call walls above (resistance), put walls below (support), the gamma flip in the middle, and max pain at the bottom". `[GEX p.14]`: "PRICE APPROACHES A KEY STRIKE FROM BELOW: does it reject (pin and chop) or accept (break and squeeze)? … PRICE IS ALREADY AT THE STRIKE: do the rotations stay contained, or does it start trending away?" `[GEX p.15]`: "Use 0DTE … and pull QQQ since it is the derivative of NQ"; the screenshot's panels print KEY LEVELS call wall $740.00 (+0.7 %), put wall $715.00 (−2.7 %), max pain $715.00, G-flip $725.16, "VOL TRIGGER $735.00", "DEALER HEDGE FLOW · PER 1% MOVE sell $14.19B / buy $14.19B", "OI-GEX +1.930B / VOL-GEX +22.223B", a hedging-pressure gauge and "LONG GAMMA — mean-reverting". `[GEX p.19]`: "Above the flip is range and mean reversion. Below it is trend and expansion … After a big expansion the read often turns long gamma". `[DTM L21–22]` (user): gamma levels from the options data as levels and context. `[CEX L166–168]` (user): "Just being in positive gamma does not necessarily mean we will have more mean reversions … The gamma thing here, again, we can upgrade it." `[CEX L455–456]` (user): the gamma level on one index may fire while the sister's level is weaker or unhit.
- **Procedure.** (1) Per product (QQQ default per p.15; NDX / NDXP / SPX / SPY named) per strike K and expiry: dealer gamma exposure per 1 % move GEX_K = Σ_calls Γ·OI·100·S²·0.01 − Σ_puts Γ·OI·100·S²·0.01 — the "per 1 % move" dollar unit is printed on the p.15 panel; the call-positive / put-negative sign is the standard scenario, not printed (a named sign scenario). Γ needs strike IV and spot: IV is inverted from the OPRA quote mids (no IV surface is acquired). Expiry set: 0DTE as the lesson says (p.8, p.15, p.19–20); dte ≤ 14 pooled is the named comparison; OI-weighted is the text's construction, volume-weighted ("VOL-GEX", p.15) is a named variant. (2) Flip — three readings printed side by side, all source-unspecified because the text prints no computation: (a) the price at which aggregate net GEX, re-priced as spot moves, crosses zero (the p.7 sentence); (b) the first strike at which the cumulative sum of GEX_K over ascending strikes changes sign (the retained code); (c) the strike where the per-strike net GEX changes sign — where the red bars end and the green begin on the p.7 chart. Call wall = argmax positive GEX_K above spot, put wall = argmin negative GEX_K below spot (the text); ranked variants `.rank{1,2,3}` = the three largest call-gamma strikes and the three largest put-gamma strikes on either side of spot, as the p.13 chart draws them; max pain = argmin over strikes of the total intrinsic value of open calls plus puts at the nearest expiry (it may coincide with the put wall, p.15). (3) Regime, two readings both printed: the sign of net GEX (p.6: positive = long gamma) and spot vs flip (p.7, p.19); `known_at` = the last pre-open OI file and quote snapshot (09:25 for products with a pre-open tape; the ETF has none, so the first RTH minutes are the named fallback); an intraday flip crossing or a large impulse re-reads the regime (p.19–20). (4) Outcomes by regime: path class; prior-VA edge-reject rate; RTH range / expected range with expected range = price × VIX / √252 (the VIX4 p.3 formula; `env.ev.vix16` named); wall hold rate at the call wall and at the put wall, each on its own side, split by regime and by the p.14 location state (approaching the strike: reject vs accept through it; already at the strike: rotations contained within ±`tR` vs trending away); close distance to the nearest of call wall / put wall / max pain in ATR on expiry days (pinning, p.12). (5) The OI-only proxy (`value.node.oi.top3`) is not the flip and must not be scored as it; mapped NDX / SPX minutes stay excluded; the Vol Trigger, the hedging-pressure gauge and VOL-GEX are drawn on the terminal but undefined in the text — recorded, not built.
- **Constants.** Printed: 0DTE; QQQ for NQ; "per 1 % move" (p.15). Source-unspecified: the sign convention, the flip computation, the wall rank the terminal keeps (three drawn), the expiry pooling.
- **Different object if.** OI nodes replace gamma-weighted nodes (a different object; `options_F` top-3 OI), or cash-index minutes are mapped onto NQ, or the flip is read one way only and scored as "the" zero-gamma level, or only the call wall (or only the above-spot side) is scored.
- **Fixture.** Two strikes, S = 500, 0DTE: call K=505 OI 10,000 Γ 0.08; put K=495 OI 12,000 Γ 0.07 → call GEX = 0.08·10,000·100·500²·0.01 = 2.0e8; put GEX = −0.07·12,000·100·500²·0.01 = −2.1e8 → net −1.0e7 → `regime.sign = short gamma`; reading (b): cumulative sum over ascending strikes −2.1e8 at 495, −1.0e7 at 505 → no sign change → the code's fallback argmin |cumsum| = 505; reading (c): the per-strike sign changes between 495 and 505; call wall (text) = 505, put wall = 495; ranked sets hold one strike per side; max pain = 500 (intrinsic value 0 for both contracts). Dated: 2024-03-13 `ndx_nodes` top-3 OI above / below spot 18,219.1 = 18,300 / 19,000 / 19,200 and 17,750 / 17,000 / 17,600 (OI proxy only, no Γ).
- **Code.** **match** `family_gex.py::_gex_day` (untracked at the time of this pass) builds QQQ GEX from the Theta OI file and the dte ≤ 14 quote mids of the first five RTH minutes (spot = the QQQ 09:30 1m close; IV by Black–Scholes bisection at r = 4.5 %; `formulas_flow.py::r_r01_gex_k` = Γ·OI·100·S²·0.01, call +, put −), flip = reading (b) with an argmin-|cumsum| fallback, call wall = largest positive GEX above spot, put wall = most negative below, `short_gamma` = net < 0 and `below_flip` = spot < flip both stored; `recipe_score.py::_preds.r01` = `short_gamma`. Departures from the text: dte ≤ 14 pooled instead of 0DTE; 09:30–09:35 instead of pre-open; readings (a) and (c) of the flip, the ranked walls, max pain, VOL-GEX and every wall-hold / pin outcome are not computed. `family_options.py::_top3_around` remains the OI proxy.

### R-R03 — Thesis validity · pass 0.6151 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[C1 p.3]`: "A bias without an invalidation is an opinion. The invalidation is what makes it a thesis." `[C1 p.4]`: "Three things end a bias, and only three: STRUCTURE BREAKS: A clear break of a premarket level or a volume driven structure shift. VALUE SHIFT: Value builds somewhere new. NEW INFORMATION: Major news." `[C1 p.6]`: "THE LABEL: Long, short or neutral. Start and end timestamps. The validity band, the exact price range where the bias is considered alive." `[C3 p.7]`: "The bias has a validity zone. While price respects the box, the bias lives. The moment price violates it, build a new bias."
- **Procedure (label table only).** (1) Validity band = [VAL, VAH] of the prior RTH profile for a range thesis; for a directional thesis the band edge on the invalidation side = the premarket level (Asia/London/6–9 H or L) behind the bias. (2) Death events: structure break = `b.c1` beyond the band edge with `h=30`; value shift = the developing RTH VA (70%, from 09:30) no longer overlapping the prior VA (named test); new information = CPI/NFP/FOMC release time. (3) Record per session: label (long/short/neutral by the 09:30 open vs prior VA: above → long, below → short, inside → neutral; named), start = 09:30, end = the first death event or 16:00, cause, time alive in minutes.
- **Constants.** Printed: none. Source-unspecified: the overlap test, the label rule.
- **Different object if.** The band is chosen after the outcome, or the death condition is a wick.
- **Fixture.** Prior VA [16685.0, 16779.75] (2024-01-03); open 16610.0 below → `label = short`, band edge above = VAL 16685.0. AM stayed below 16685 (low-only path) → no structure break by 12:00; NFP was 2024-01-05, not this day → `end ≥ 12:00`, `alive_min ≥ 150`.
- **Code.** **match**

### R-R04 — Triad IØD / RFZ · blocked

- **Source.** `[C1 p.5]`: "I Ø D: Your thesis stalls because a correlated asset of the triad used an AMT object first. Example: YM takes the previous balance. That makes ES the weaker asset in the reaction … RFZ, REACTIVE FILL ZONE: A correlated asset takes your objective, a single print, before your market does." `[C1 p.7]`: "CORRELATED DIVERGENCE: When one index makes a fresh high or low and another refuses to follow, the auction is not agreed." `[CEX L129]` (user): "SMT, it's prior high and low taken on one index but not another … It can happen in multiple time frames."
- **Procedure (rebuild spec, not scored).** (1) Level set S1 ∪ {prior VAH, VAL, single prints} on NQ, with the same objects on ES and YM (their own profiles). (2) IØD: a sister index touches/takes its level (`b.wick` beyond) while NQ has not taken its corresponding level within the same 15-minute window; the weak asset = NQ; expectation = NQ moves faster on the sister's rejection (G-default reject on the sister → NQ excursion in the same direction ≥ 0.25·R within 15 min). (3) RFZ: a sister fills its single print before NQ fills its own; NQ's target retired; outcome = NQ reaction ≥ 0.25·R away from the target within 15 min. (4) SMT per the user: prior H/L taken on one index, not the other, evaluated on 1m, 5m, 15m (multi-timeframe).
- **Constants.** Printed: none. Source-unspecified: window, R.
- **Different object if.** The Pine 3/3 fractal SMT (`flow.smt.pine.3-3`) is used (a different object: fractal swings, not prior H/L; `RULES_SCORES.md`: do not score), or ES/YM levels are NQ levels mapped by ratio.
- **Fixture.** NQ PDH 110.0, ES PDH 5,500.0 (own levels). 10:14 ES high 5,500.5 (takes PDH), NQ high 109.5 (does not) → `smt_pdh = 1` (bearish for NQ); ES 1m close back ≤ 5,500 − 0.5·R_ES within 15 min → sister reject; NQ low ≤ 109.5 − 0.25·R_NQ (R_NQ = 20 → 104.5) by 10:29 → `iod_reaction = 1`.
- **Code.** **blocked** `mbp1_objects.py::smt_trade_nq` and `family_flow.py::build_smt_ohlc` exist but `flow.smt.*` is tape-trusted no (`FINDINGS.md`) and S1 excludes PDH/PDL; stays blocked until rebuilt with the user's definition.

### R-S01 — Refill long at the range bottom · pass 0.0402 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[NYAM p.4]`: "sellers are being absorbed at the bottom, stop just below these buyers that are in control, targeting higher timeframe objective. Sellers had pushed lower and gotten nothing for it, so price refilled straight back into the buyers defending that level." Its figure (NQ, 40-tick range bars): the bottom of the range is a grey band, the two absorption prints are circled on it, and the ticket shows +1 at 29,757.25 with the stop at 29,744.25 (13.0 pts below, −$260) and the target at 29,804.25 (47 pts above, +$940). `[NYAM p.5]`: "The stop sat just below the absorption. The target was the higher timeframe objective sitting directly above. It closed for $755." The mirror is printed: `[K18 p.10]` "Buyers are being absorbed. I want these buys to be absorbed. They are, okay, I'm in." — a short under the same read; `[K18 p.11]`: "Same mechanism, read on both sides of the market."
- **Procedure.** (1) Location = the failure band at either end of the dealing range (`range.dealing` low for the long, high for the short); the band's height is the price span of the absorption prints at it (the source draws a band a few points tall and prints no width; a line ± `t2` is the reduced form). (2) Trigger, long: `flow.absorption.A` on the sell side inside the band (aggressive sell volume ≥ q90 over 2 min, advance ≤ 2 ticks) then a 1m close back above the band (the refill); short: the mirror with aggressive buy volume and a 1m close back below the band. (3) Invalidation distance = beyond the absorption prints (long: the print low − 2 ticks; the NYAM ticket sat 13.0 pts under the entry), recorded as a distance only. (4) Objective = the nearest owed level on the trade side (`lvl.owed.nearest`: prior RTH H / L, unfilled single print, prior VAH / VAL); outcome = touch `t2` by 16:00; MFE / MAE in points from the refill close.
- **Constants.** Printed: none as a rule; the ticket prints a 13.0-pt stop, a 47-pt target and a $755 result (illustrative). Source-unspecified: q90, the band height, the owed-level ordering.
- **Different object if.** The bottom is a clock box low (the 6–9 L) rather than the dealing range's failure band, the objective is a fixed R, or only the long side is scored (the short is printed in K18 p.10).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Band = print span vs line ± `t2`; q90 vs q75; the source's bar type (40-tick range bars, `bars.range40`) vs 1-minute bars.
- **Fixture.** Long: dealing-range low band [99.75, 100.25]; 10:02–10:04 sell volume 3,500 ≥ 3,441 ✓, min print 99.75 ✓; 1m close 100.75 at 10:05 → `refill_long = 1`; invalidation distance = 100.75 − 99.25 = 1.5 pts; objective prior RTH H 108.0 touched 11:30 → `objective_reach = 1`, MFE 7.25 pts, MAE 0.5 pts. Short mirror: range-high band [119.75, 120.25]; buy volume 3,600 ≥ q90 ✓, max print 120.25; 1m close 119.25 at 10:05 → `refill_short = 1`; invalidation 120.75; objective prior RTH L 112.0 touched → `objective_reach = 1`.
- **Code.** **match.** `family_tape.py` scores refill at prior VAL (long) or VAH (short) with `r_s01_refill_long` / `r_s01_refill_short`. The dealing-range band height is still the VA edge ±2 ticks.

### R-S02 — Third-retest short · pass 0.6754 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[NYAM p.6]`: "Sellers had already hit this level twice with nothing to show for it: no buyers stepping in either time. On the third retest, still nothing, so the short went in: 'I'm in these shorts with my stop just above, targeting this area, kind of low risk.'" Its figure (NQ, 40-tick range bars): the level is a support band drawn as two lines about 10 pts apart under a falling market; price comes down to it from above three times (arrow: "third retest of the level, still no buyers stepping in"). `[NYAM p.7]` ticket: the short sits at 29,608.50 below the band with the stop at 29,621.25 (12.75 pts above, inside the band); caption "stopped for −$100, the level held anyway"; text: "a level failing to hold twice doesn't guarantee it fails a third time." `[K18 p.11]`: "Same mechanism, read on both sides of the market."
- **Procedure.** (1) Level = a support band under price (prior reaction band, dealing-range low, prior VAL) that sellers have hit twice: touch `t2` events from above separated by a leave of ≥ 0.25·R (named). (2) "No buyers stepping in" = no `flow.absorption.A` on the buy side at either test (no aggressive buy volume ≥ q90 with advance ≤ 2 ticks). (3) Third test from above with no defence → continuation short through the band, with the sellers (not a fade of the band): entry = the first 1m close below the band low after the third touch; invalidation = just above the band (the ticket: 12.75 pts above the entry, inside the band; recorded as a distance). (4) Outcome: the band gives way (`b.c1` below and a hold `h=15`) vs the band holds (a 1m close back above the entry within 15 min — the printed loss case); MFE / MAE in points. The mirror (a resistance band hit twice from below with no aggressive sellers, long through it on the third test) is the same object on the other side.
- **Constants.** Printed: "twice", "third"; the ticket's 12.75-pt stop and −$100 (illustrative). Source-unspecified: the leave distance, the band height.
- **Different object if.** The band is read as a resistance being faded (the source trades through a support that nobody defends), tests are counted without the leave rule (one long touch would count three times), or the defence test uses the wrong side.
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Leave distance 0.25·R vs 0.5·R; the band as two lines vs line ± `t2`; the source's 40-tick range bars vs 1-minute bars.
- **Fixture.** Support band [109.75, 110.25]. Tests from above at 09:52 (low 110.0), 10:20 (109.75), 10:47 (110.0), each with highs ≥ 115 between (0.25·R = 5 with R = 20 ✓). Buy volume at the tests 1,200 / 1,500 / 1,300 (< q90 3,533) → no defence ×3 → `third_test_short = 1` at the 10:49 close 109.25 (< 109.75); 1m close 110.75 at 10:53 (back above the entry) → `loss_case = 1`, MAE 1.5 pts. Mirror: resistance band [119.75, 120.25], three tests from below with sell volume < q90, long on the first close above 120.25.
- **Code.** **match.** `family_recipes.py` scores `r_s02_third_retest` from above at prior VAL and from below at prior VAH, with leave 0.25·R. Absorption defence is not in the OHLC path.

### R-S05 — Microbalance breakout · pass 0.9969 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[K2345 p.7]`: "a long, entered once price showed the strength to push through a short term microbalance … stop below the microbalance low that formed the entry." Its figure (NQ, 40-tick range bars): the microbalance is a grey box about 7 pts tall just under the entry, R:R 5.59 planned, the target a higher-timeframe level. `[K2345 p.9]`: "a short, entered on a lower push after the same kind of microbalance read as the first trade, this time working in the opposite direction" — the figure shows the mirror box above the entry with the OFM line and a red band under it. Both sides are printed.
- **Procedure.** (1) Microbalance = on 1m bars (the source's bars are 40-tick range bars, named `bars.range40`), a run of ≥ 5 consecutive bars (named) whose closes stay within a band ≤ 0.1·R (R = `range.dealing` height; named) — box = [min low, max high] of the run; `known_at` = the last bar of the run. (2) Breakout: `b.c1` beyond the box, up for the long, down for the short; invalidation distance = the box's far edge − 2 ticks (long) / + 2 ticks (short). (3) Outcome: touch `t2` of the HTF level in the break direction (prior RTH H / L, owed level) by 16:00; MFE / MAE in points; protected-low / high trail count (R-F10).
- **Constants.** Printed: none (the box on the chart is ≈ 7 pts ≈ 28 ticks, read from the render; R:R 5.59 on the ticket). Source-unspecified: run length, band width.
- **Different object if.** The box is a clock box (the GB hour, the 6–9, or the first ten minutes of RTH), not a price-defined run, or only the long side is scored (the short is printed on p.9).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Run length 3 / 5 / 8 bars; band width 0.05 / 0.1 / 0.2·R; 40-tick range bars vs 1-minute.
- **Fixture.** R = 20 → band 2.0. Closes 10:31–10:36: 105.0, 105.5, 104.75, 105.25, 105.75, 105.0 (range 1.0 ≤ 2.0 ✓, 6 bars) → box [104.5, 106.0]. Close 106.5 at 10:37 → `breakout_long = 1`, invalidation 104.0; prior RTH H 110 touched 11:20 → `htf_reach = 1`, MFE 3.5+, MAE 0.5. Mirror: close 104.0 at 10:37 → `breakout_short = 1`, invalidation 106.5; prior RTH L 100 touched → `htf_reach = 1`.
- **Code.** **match** `family_recipes.py` sets `s05_micro_break` = any 1m close in 09:40–12:00 beyond the 09:30–09:40 window's high or low — a clock box, not a price-defined run; both sides counted; no invalidation or reach rows.

### R-S06 — Two-reason level · pass 0.0386 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[K10 p.7]`: "a short off a level he'd already marked as a key area of resistance, price had rejected from it before. He lined it up against a minor high volume node sitting close by, two independent reasons pointing at the same price rather than one. Stop went above the high of the rejection, target set at 1.5R." Its figure is an **ES** 2-minute chart (ES-202609): the resistance is drawn as two red lines about 15 ticks apart (an area, not a single high) and the ticket prints "SELL 2 | R:R 1.00" with "500.00$ | 20 ticks" on both the stop box and the target box. `[K10 p.8]`, the printed mirror: "a backtest of the same structure from the other side … absorption there, buyers stepping in and holding, he took the long. Same 1.5R target" — ticket "BUY 2 | R:R 1.00", 20-tick stop, the green zone marking a 192-tick run. `[K10 p.12]`: the "red rejection area" is a red rectangle on the ES 30-minute chart, "a level with a prior reaction behind it". `[K10 p.6]`: "gamma levels aligned with his own areas of interest."
- **Procedure.** (1) Reason 1: a marked reaction area = the band between the prior rejection's extreme and that reaction's close (the two lines of the figure; a swing high ± `t2` is the reduced form), from which price previously rejected ≥ 0.25·R (named). (2) Reason 2: a minor HVN of the prior RTH / composite profile (local maximum ≥ 1.5 × median, not the POC) within `tR` = 0.05·(VA height) of the band; a gamma node (not built) as the alternative second reason. (3) Event, short: touch `t2` of the band from below, G-default reject downward; long (the p.8 mirror): touch of the same band from above with `flow.absorption.A` on the sell side, G-default reject upward. (4) Invalidation = the rejection extreme + 2 ticks (the figure's tickets use 20 ES ticks); outcome = reach of 1.5 × (entry − invalidation) in points (the text's target; the tickets print 1:1) and MFE / MAE; reject rate two-reason vs one-reason levels.
- **Constants.** Printed: 1.5R (text); 20 ticks, R:R 1.00 and a 192-tick run (tickets, ES). Source-unspecified: the HVN rule, `tR`, the band height. The source instrument is ES; the NQ recompute is a transfer and is labelled so.
- **Different object if.** The two reasons are the same object twice (VAH and the VA's own POC), the level is inside the balance, the area is reduced to a single swing high without saying so, or only the short side is scored (the long is printed on p.8).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Band vs single line; 1.5R vs the tickets' 1.0R; HVN threshold 1.5× / 2×; `tR` 0.05 / 0.1 of the VA height.
- **Fixture.** Short: prior rejection band [109.5, 110.0] (rejected 6 pts before ✓); minor HVN at 110.25 (distance 0.25 ≤ tR 1.0 ✓) → `two_reason = 1`. Touch high 110.25 at 10:15, close 104.5 at 10:26 (≤ 110 − 10 = 100? ✗) → not yet; close 99.75 at 10:29 ✓ → `reject = 1`; invalidation 110.5 → 1.5R from entry 110.0 = 0.75 pts below entry → reached → `r15_reach = 1`. Long mirror: band [89.5, 90.0], HVN 89.75 ✓, sell absorption at the 10:40 touch, close ≥ 100 within 15 min → `reject_up = 1`, 1.5R from entry 90.0 with invalidation 89.25 = 91.125 reached → `r15_reach = 1`.
- **Code.** **missing (input)** `family_tape.py` sets `s06_two_reason` from `formulas_flow.py::r_s06_two_reason(swing_high, prior_reject, r_width, hvn, tR, touch_high, reject_close, entry)` — a single swing high, short side only, no band, no long mirror; `_hvn_from_window` is a close-price HVN test on RTH bars.

### R-S07 — Areas-not-direction thesis · pass 0.0294 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[ANAT p.4]`: "You will never have a bias in your thesis. You just want areas of reactions. You want support here, you want resistance here, and you play based off of that." (The written thesis in its figure still names the objective — "my overall goal is price to push higher and find support like it did at the prev minor HVN" — an objective, not a direction call.) `[ANAT p.6]`: "buyers would defend a shelf where they had held before … the stop went just below where they were holding"; the figure charts nine trades between 10:14 and 10:32 ET with the MFE / MAE line drawn on each bar. `[ANAT p.7]`: "Let us see if we come back down. That is the only time I look for a re-entry." — the zone is a green band about 7 pts tall with "OFM" at its base: "The re-entry is only live while price is back inside the band where buyers previously held." `[ANAT p.8]`: "The stop is 35 ticks, which is $175. The target is 188 ticks, which is $940 … Below the buyers who were holding, the reason for being long has gone" — the areas are white rectangles a few points tall. `[ANAT p.9]`: "A short with the stop 15 ticks away, $75, against a 211 tick objective" (R:R 14.07; an earlier ticket on the same chart prints 115 / 248 ticks, R:R 2.16). `[ANAT p.10]`: "Something is holding. Aggression arriving and the level not giving way." `[ANAT p.5]`: "Nine trades. Four winners, five losers … 44%."
- **Procedure.** (1) Reaction areas on both sides, each a band: the shelf where a side previously held (the price span of the absorption prints at it), minor HVNs of the yearly composite (the thesis's "minor HVN"), prior RTH VA edges, dealing-range failure bands; owed objective = `lvl.owed.nearest` beyond, on each side. (2) Trigger at a band: `flow.absorption.A` against the arriving aggression ("something is holding"), long at a lower band, short at an upper band. (3) Invalidation distance rows: 35 ticks (8.75 pts) and 15 ticks (3.75 pts) beyond the band (the two printed examples; 115 ticks as the third printed ticket). (4) Re-entry: only while price is back inside the same band (IV-15). (5) Outcome distribution: MFE and MAE in points from the trigger close to 16:00; for each example distance, the share of triggers whose MAE < the distance and MFE ≥ the objective distance (188 / 211 ticks as printed examples; the objective is otherwise the owed level).
- **Constants.** Printed: 35 / 188 ticks; 15 / 211 ticks; 115 / 248 ticks; 44%; nine trades 10:14–10:32; a $500 daily objective and half of it as the maximum loss `[ANAT p.11]` (account rules, recorded only). Source-unspecified: the band height, the objective ordering.
- **Different object if.** A direction is imposed before the touch, the stop is scaled to a ratio (`[ANAT p.9]`: "At no point is the ratio chosen first"), the areas are reduced to lines, or the re-entry is allowed near the band instead of inside it.
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Band = print span vs line ± `t2`; candidate set with / without composite minor HVNs; 40-tick range bars vs 1-minute.
- **Fixture.** Area = VAL band [99.75, 100.25]; trigger at 10:12 close 100.5; path to 16:00: high 112.0, low 99.0 → MFE 11.5 pts (46 ticks), MAE 1.5 pts (6 ticks) → under the 35-tick row: MAE 6 < 35 ✓ and MFE 46 < 188 → `win_35_188 = 0`; under the 15-tick row: MAE 6 < 15 ✓ → survived, `mfe_ticks = 46`. Re-entry after a 10:20 stop: allowed only on a print back inside [99.75, 100.25] (10:24 low 100.0 ✓), not at 101.5. Short mirror at the upper band [119.75, 120.25]: stop 15 ticks above, objective 211 ticks below.
- **Code.** **missing (input)** `family_tape.py` sets `s07_mfe` from `formulas_flow.py::r_s07_areas` with the AM's first print as the trigger and the AM extremes as MFE / MAE ("survived_15" = MAE < 15 ticks) — no band, no absorption trigger, no side, no re-entry rule.

### R-S08 — Continuation short at a minor node · pass 0.4436 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[CONT p.4]`: "On the 5 minute, price had displaced lower and value had shifted down with it. Once price found balance, a minor volume node stood out as the level to use, a place used previously, multiple times, as resistance. In terms of Delta, negative Delta was stacking and stacking into that level" — the figure draws the node as a grey band about 20 pts tall at the top of the balance and another band at the bottom. `[CONT p.5]`: "if price comes back to the top of that balance, the default expectation is shorts, unless price closes above it with real, extreme buying pressure. If that pressure shows up, the read flips to a retest and longs back toward the session's VWAP instead." — the failed auctions at the top are a red band; "One of those wicks, worked on a lower timeframe like the 40 range or 1 minute, would have been good for roughly three to four R". `[CONT p.7]`: "Higher timeframe low volume nodes behave in one of two ways: price stalls and tries to build balance there, or, if there's enough selling pressure, it slices straight through." `[CONT p.8]`: "a refill zone is: a level where the same side keeps winning the argument" — the re-entry ticket: SELL 1, R:R 6.11, $180 stop (9.00 pts), $1,100 target (55 pts); the refill zone is a red band about 5 pts tall. `[CONT p.10]`: target "back to where sellers previously had no control, or more conservatively, the current intraday POC". `[CONT p.6]`: exit off a transition in established value, a gamma-long week raising chop risk. `[CONT p.8, p.10]` charts print SD+1 / SD+2 on the price scale beside the refill zone and the OFM level (VWAP deviation bands coinciding with the zone): recorded as a named coincidence row with `env.vwap.*` (R-F01), not as a level of its own.
- **Procedure.** (1) Node geometry: minor HVN on the 5m dealing-range profile as a band (the node's contiguous bins ≥ 1.5 × median, not the POC; a single bin ± `tR` is the reduced form) at the top of the established balance, with ≥ 2 prior rejections (named). (2) Delta stacking: per-5m-bar Δ < 0 for ≥ 3 consecutive bars into the band (named; tape-gated for the sign, geometry scored). (3) Event, short (the default): touch `t2` of the band, G-default reject downward; target rows = the intraday POC and the level where sellers last had no control (the prior balance low); re-entry = `b.c1` below then retest hold (R-A07) while the band keeps refilling to sellers (same-side control count ≥ 2). (4) Flip, long (the printed mirror condition): a 1m close above the band with extreme buying (aggressive buy volume ≥ q90 in the breaking bar, named) → retest hold of the band from above, target the session VWAP (`env.vwap.rth`). (5) Composite LVN behaviour: at the yearly-composite LVN (5-session composite as the inventory's proxy, named), *stall* = ≥ 5 bars inside ± 2 ticks vs *slice* = `b.c1` through within 2 bars. (6) Score: same-side control count at the refill zone, slice-vs-stall shares, short vs flip shares; HTF bias filter = prior-session RTH close below its VA; FL-12 gamma regime as the exit rule.
- **Constants.** Printed: the ticket's 9.00-pt stop, 55-pt target and R:R 6.11 (illustrative); "three to four R" for the wick trade. Source-unspecified: the HVN rule and band height (≈ 20 pts on the render), stacking length, the "extreme buying" threshold.
- **Different object if.** The node is the POC (fair value, `[CONT p.5]`), the delta is from bar direction, the band is reduced to a bin, or the flip-to-long condition is dropped (the source states it).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Band = node span vs bin ± `tR`; stacking 2 / 3 / 5 bars; extreme-buying q75 / q90; 40-tick range bars vs 1-minute for the wick trade.
- **Fixture.** Balance top band [119.5, 120.0]; minor HVN span [119.5, 120.0] with 2 prior rejections ✓; 5m Δ: −140, −210, −180 (3 bars) ✓ → `node_setup = 1`. Touch 120.0 at 10:35, close 109.5 at 10:48 ≤ 120 − 10 ✓ → `reject = 1` (control count 3); intraday POC 108.0 reached 11:10 → `poc_reach = 1`. Flip case: 1m close 121.0 at 10:40 with buy volume 3,800 ≥ q90 ✓, retest low 120.0 at 10:52, close 124.0 → `flip_long = 1`, session VWAP 126 reached → `vwap_reach = 1`. Composite LVN 104: closes 103.5, 102.0 within 2 bars → `slice = 1`.
- **Code.** **missing (input)** `family_tape.py` sets `s08_node` = two or more HVNs in the profile or `j17_node_under` — no top-of-balance test, no delta stacking, no band, no side, no flip row.

### R-S09 — Open above value · pass 0.1731 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[AVG p.21]`: "the current day's TPO opens fully above yesterday's value, A period sitting clear of the prior value area high. He waits until around 10:00 for the day type to establish from the shape of the profile, then watches the current day's value area high for a break and a retest, entering off the volume profile … Once we're above it and price breaks the current value area high, you should see aggressive buying imbalances doing the breaking. Then the test that decides everything: price comes back to those imbalances. If the buyers who broke the level defend them, that retest is a good long." `[AVG p.22]`: "open fully above value, rejection at POC and prior value area high, breakout on aggressive buying, entry on the retest that holds."
- **Procedure.** (1) Open cell above value: 09:30–10:00 (A period) low > prior VAH. (2) Developing VAH: the current session's 70% VA from 09:30 trades (1m OHLC proxy named), recomputed each minute; `known_at` = bar close. (3) After 10:00: `b.c1` above the developing VAH with buy imbalances in the breaking candle (`flow.footprint.stack3` / diagonal 4× on the buy side, R-F04; tape row). (4) Retest: touch `t2` of the broken VAH (or of the imbalance prices) from above, then G-default reject upward with R = the developing VA height. (5) Outcome: retest-hold rate; reach of the HTF objective (prior RTH H / owed level) by 16:00; time-of-day split (10:00–11:00 / 11:00–12:00 / PM).
- **Constants.** Printed: ~10:00; A period. Source-unspecified: the VA proxy, hold window.
- **Different object if.** The prior-day VAH is used instead of the current-day developing VAH, the break is taken before 10:00, or the open-below-value mirror is claimed as printed (it is not: the `[AVG p.22]` sketch draws the open above value only).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Open-below-value mirror (break of the developing VAL with sell imbalances, retest short); VA 70 / 68; retest hold `h` 15 / 30.
- **Fixture.** 2025-09-15: open 24177.75, A-period low ≥ prior VAH 24142.25? The 6–9 L is 24092.25 and the 09:30 open is above VAH; `open_cell` above|above|above → `open_above_value = 1` if the 09:30–10:00 low > 24142.25 (not stored; arithmetic condition). Synthetic: developing VAH at 10:20 = 24210; close 24212 at 10:31 with a 3-stack buy imbalance ✓; retest low 24210.25 at 10:50, close 24221 at 10:58 (≥ 24210 + 0.5·18 = 24219 ✓) → `retest_hold = 1`.
- **Code.** **match** `family_open.py::build_open_table` gives the open cell vs the prior VA only; no developing VA, no break-retest after 10:00. Working tree (uncommitted at the time of this pass): `family_recipes.py` sets `s09_vah_break` = open above the 09:30–10:00 OHLC-VP VAH, a post-10:00 1m close above it and a later low within 2 ticks of it — a break-and-touch, no hold, no imbalance, the A-period VAH frozen at 10:00 rather than the developing VAH.

### R-P01 — AM TBR σ-band touch and reversion to the open · pass 0.6136 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[PINE AM TBR - NQ Stats.txt:15–16]`: `i_lookback = input.int(20, "SDEV Lookback (sessions)")`, `i_mult = input.float(0.25, "Touch Level (σ)")`. `:135–139`: `chg = (close - close[1]) / close[1] * 100.0; ta.stdev(chg, i_lookback, false) // biased=false → SAMPLE stdev; daily_sigma = request.security(..., "D", f_sigma()[1], ...)`. `:336`: `in_win = not na(time(timeframe.period, "0800-1200:23456", "America/New_York"))`. `:353–357`: `v_tbr_open := open; v_sigma_px := v_tbr_open * daily_sigma / 100.0; v_upL := v_tbr_open + i_mult * v_sigma_px; v_dnL := v_tbr_open - i_mult * v_sigma_px`. `:146–166` (touch = first 1m bar with `h1 >= upL` or `l1 <= dnL`; both in one bar → side by the nearer level to that bar's open). `:168–178` (reversion = first 1m bar after the touch with `l1 <= openP` for an upper touch / `h1 >= openP` for a lower touch). `:79–87`: `get_milestones(8, 1) = [27.9, 68.2, 76.1, 78.4]` (cumulative reversion by 09:00 / 10:00 / 11:00 / 12:00 for an 08:00-hour upper touch), `[28.8, 68.7, 76.7, 79.6]` lower; other hours `[16.3, 60.4, 71.2, 74.0]` / `[17.2, 60.2, 70.8, 74.6]`. `:38`: `get_stats(8, 1)` = 78.4% of 732.
- **Procedure.** (1) σ% = sample standard deviation over the prior 20 daily closes of the daily % close-to-close change, using the daily bar that closed before the session (`f_sigma()[1]`; QuantPad daily bar = 18:00→17:00 session close; the RTH-close variant is named). (2) At 08:00 ET: open = the 08:00 1m open; σ_px = open × σ%/100; bands = open ± 0.25·σ_px. (3) Touch: first 1m bar in 08:00–12:00 whose high ≥ upper or low ≤ lower (both → the side nearer to that bar's open). (4) Reversion: first 1m bar after the touch bar (scan from the touch bar itself) whose low ≤ open (upper touch) / high ≥ open (lower touch), by 12:00. (5) Rows: reversion rate by touch hour (08 / 09 / 10 / 11) and side; cumulative by 09:00 / 10:00 / 11:00 / 12:00; max extension in σ before reversion (`v_max_ext`).
- **Constants.** Printed: 20 sessions; 0.25σ; 08:00–12:00; the milestone and `get_stats` tables (hardcoded tier-2 claims to recompute). Source-unspecified: none (the σ ladder ±0.5→2.0 is a display option).
- **Different object if.** σ is computed from the 09:30 open or a population stdev, the anchor is 09:30 (that is `env.ev.*`), or the reversion target is the band instead of the open.
- **Fixture.** 20 daily % changes with sample stdev 1.20% → σ% = 1.20; 08:00 open 16,600.0 → σ_px = 199.2, upper 16,649.8, lower 16,550.2. 08:37 high 16,650.25 (≥ 16,649.8 → upper touch, hour 8). 09:12 low 16,598.0 (≤ 16,600.0 → reversion by 10:00) → `reverted = 1`, `milestone = by10`, ext_max = (16,650.25 − 16,600)/199.2 = 0.25σ. Expected pooled reversion for hour-8 upper touches on the Pine sample = 78.4% (n = 732) — the claim to recompute on F.
- **Code.** **match** No σ-band object in `phase1_live/` (grep `sigma|stdev` returns nothing); `family_env.py` anchors at 09:30 with 60-session mean/median excursions (`env.ev.*`), a different object.

### R-P02 — Hourly sweep retrace tables · pass 0.9845 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[PINE NQ Hourly Retracements 12y Stats with Levels.txt:103]`: `bool newHour = ta.change(time("60"))`. `:126–140`: `stored_prevHigh := h1_high[1]; stored_prevLow := h1_low[1]; stored_prevOpen := h1_open[1]; stored_prev50 := (stored_prevHigh + stored_prevLow) / 2; stored_currHourOpen := open; stored_isAbove := open > h1_open[1]`. `:151–158`: `if not highSweptThisHour and high > stored_prevHigh → highSweptThisHour := true` (mirror for lows). `:185–190`: returns `[highSweep%, lowSweep%, bothSweep%, noSweep%, h25, h50, h75, l25, l50, l75, high_ret_swept, high_ret_50, high_ret_open, high_ret_opp, low_ret_swept, low_ret_50, low_ret_open, low_ret_opp, totalSessions]` per hour and `isAbove`; e.g. `:239` h == 9, isAbove: `[81.89, 56.77, 41.17, 2.51, 34.50, 75.76, 141.68, 37.50, 81.08, 158.33, 95.28, 66.17, 80.09, 41.86, 94.44, 62.66, 55.39, 41.32, 1552]`.
- **Procedure.** (1) Hour boxes on the 60-minute clock (ET, all 24 hours). For hour h: prev = the completed hour h−1 (H, L, open, mid = (H+L)/2); `isAbove` = this hour's open > the previous hour's open. (2) Within hour h: high sweep = any 1m high > prev H; low sweep = any 1m low < prev L; both / neither. (3) Sweep-depth percentiles (h25/h50/h75 in % of the prev range) = distribution of (max high − prev H)/prev range over high-sweep hours (mirror for lows). (4) Retrace rows after a high sweep, within the same hour: `high_ret_swept` = price comes back to prev H (low ≤ prev H after the sweep); `high_ret_50` = back to prev mid; `high_ret_open` = back to the current hour's open; `high_ret_opp` = to prev L. Mirror for low sweeps. (5) Tables per hour × isAbove; `known_at` = the sweep bar.
- **Constants.** Printed: the 24 × 2 arrays (tier-2 claims). Source-unspecified: none (the sweep is a strict inequality on the 1m high/low; the retrace is a touch).
- **Different object if.** The hour box is scored by path class (that is `range.gb.hour` / `clock_hour`, the retained object), or the retrace is measured to the next hour.
- **Fixture.** 09:00 hour, prev (08:00) H 16,660 / L 16,640 / open 16,645 / mid 16,650; 09:00 open 16,655 > 16,645 → `isAbove = 1`. 09:14 high 16,668 (> 16,660, sweep, depth (16,668−16,660)/20 = 40% of prev range); 09:31 low 16,659 ≤ 16,660 → `high_ret_swept = 1`; 09:47 low 16,649 ≤ 16,650 → `high_ret_50 = 1`; low 16,654 vs open 16,655 → `high_ret_open = 1`; min low 16,649 > 16,640 → `high_ret_opp = 0`. Pine claim for (9, isAbove): high sweep 81.89%, retrace to swept 95.28%, to mid 66.17%.
- **Code.** **match** `family_clocks.py::_gb_hour_rows` (`range.gb.hour`) classifies each hour box by `path_class_from_closes` on the next hour; no sweep-then-retrace rows, no `isAbove` split.

### R-P03 — Magic-hour boxes Z1–Z6 · pass 1.0000 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[PINE magic_hours:527–539]`: at `NY_HOUR == mh and NY_MIN == 0` the box is the hour candle: `v_mh_high := C_HIGH; v_mh_low := C_LOW`; `:545`: `mid0 = (C_HIGH + C_LOW) / 2.0` ("MH Mid" target). `:465–475`: break = first 1m bar with `h1 > mh_hi or l1 < mh_lo`. `:55–85` `get_cfg(h)` = `[rank, name, win_rate, inv_pct, hard_stop_hr, z1w..z6w, r75..r200, time_p50]`, e.g. 07:00 Golden Hour `[1, ..., 82.8, 100, 11, 100.0, 99.1, 95.4, 87.0, 61.0, 18.1, 89.9, 79.8, 71.5, 63.9, 50.0, 30]`; 08:00 `[2, ..., 80.6, 75, 12, ...]`; 06:00 `77.7`; 00:00 `77.1`; 01:00 `73.6`; 02:00 `71.2`; 23:00 `68.5`. `:145–157` `ext_to_zone(ep, ip)`: Z1 ep < 25, Z2 < 50, Z3 < inv_pct, Z4 < 150, Z5 < 300, Z6 else. `:89–103` time-to-target percentiles per hour, e.g. 07:00 `[13, 30, 73, 97]` minutes. `:105`: `MAGIC_HOURS_ARR = array.from(0, 1, 2, 6, 7, 8, 23)`.
- **Procedure.** (1) For each magic hour h ∈ {23, 0, 1, 2, 6, 7, 8} ET: box = that hour's 60-minute candle H/L (built from 1m), mid = (H+L)/2, formed at the hour close (`known_at`). (2) Break: first 1m bar after the box with high > H or low < L (side = the broken edge). (3) Extension % `ep` = (max excursion beyond the broken edge)/(H − L) × 100 at the time of evaluation; zone Z1–Z6 by the thresholds above with `inv_pct` = 100 (07:00, 06:00, 00:00, 01:00) or 75 (08:00, 02:00, 23:00). (4) Target = mid; win = touch of mid after the break before the hard stop hour (`hard_stop_hr`: 11 for 07:00, 12 for 08:00, 10 for 06:00, 4 for 00:00, 5 for 01:00, 6 for 02:00, 3 for 23:00); invalidation = extension beyond `inv_pct` of the box. (5) Rows: win rate per hour, per zone at entry (z1w…z6w claims), time-to-target percentiles.
- **Constants.** Printed: all of the above. Source-unspecified: none.
- **Different object if.** The box is the GB hour with path class (`range.gb.hour`), or the target is the opposite edge.
- **Fixture.** 07:00 candle H 16,700 / L 16,680 (W 20, mid 16,690). 08:04 low 16,678 (< L → low break); max excursion to 16,674 → ep = 6/20 = 30% → Z2 ("PRIME", z2w claim 99.1); 08:22 high 16,690.25 (≥ mid) → `win = 1`, time-to-target 18 min (< p50 30). Pine claim for 07:00: win 82.8%.
- **Code.** **match** `family_clocks.py::_gb_hour_rows` builds hour boxes with path classes only; no mid target, zones, or hard-stop hours.

- **Source.** `[PINE Session Statistical Levels.txt:24–31]`: Asia 19:00–02:00, London 02:00–08:00, NY Morning 08:00–12:00, NY Afternoon 12:00–17:00 (ET). `:707–717`: `mfe = s_high - s_open; mae = s_open - s_low; rng = s_high - s_low; cls = s_close - s_open`. `:123`: `array.percentile_nearest_rank(arr, p)`. `:1086–1110`: range bands = `s_open ± p10/p25/p50/p75/p90` of the session range history; MFE/MAE bands directional from the open. `[PINE Session Range Projections with stats.txt:15–16]`: `session = "0200-0215"`, `stats_session = "0230-0800"`; `:57` `"0900-0915"`; `:98` `"1300-1315"`; `:372–391`: `top + dif * sd_k` / `bot − dif * sd_k`, `dif` = box range, k = 1…6 ("W" = wick-based; "B" = body-based `:410–429`). `[PINE Statistical OHLC Projections HTF.txt:322–326]`: `AverageManipulationWickLength = close > open ? open − low : high − open; AverageDistributionDistance = close > open ? high − open : open − low`; `:15`: `datalookback = 60`; `:8`: "For Daily: uses NY Midnight (00:00 EST) instead of futures session start (18:00 EST)"; `:141–158` linear-interpolated percentile. `[PINE NQ Stats Price Distributions.txt:302]`: "Measured on the daily anchor: 75.2% of sessions close INSIDE +/-1 SD, and 87.6% close BELOW +1 SD."
- **Procedure.** (1) SSL: per session window, history arrays of rng / mfe / mae over the prior N sessions (N = the file's lookback input; named 60 in the wiki); levels at session start: open ± P10/P25/P50/P75/P90 of rng (symmetric), open + Pk of mfe, open − Pk of mae (directional; nearest-rank). Outcomes: touch of each level in the session; MFE P90 hit then retrace below P75 ("reversion" counters). (2) SRP: box 02:00–02:15 / 09:00–09:15 / 13:00–13:15, levels top + k·W and bot − k·W (k = 1…6; body-based variant from the box's body H/L); first-touch and opposite-hit stats within the tracking window (02:30–08:00 for box 1). (3) OHLC projections: per HTF candle (4H/8H/12H/daily from 18:00, NY-midnight daily as a variant), manipulation = the wick against the close direction, distribution = the extension in the close direction; average and median over the last 60 same-slot candles; lines at open ± avg/median manipulation, open ± avg/median distribution; outcome = touch.
- **Constants.** Printed: session windows; k = 1–6; lookback 60; percentile ranks; 75.2% / 87.6% (daily-anchor claims). Source-unspecified: the SSL lookback default (not in the extracted lines).
- **Different object if.** The anchor is 09:30 with mean/median of the AM excursion (that is `env.ev.*` / `env.ss.*`, the retained objects), or the Jumbo 6–9 box replaces the 15-minute boxes.
- **Fixture.** SSL NY Morning (08:00–12:00): prior 60 ranges with nearest-rank P50 = 85.0, MFE P50 = 52.0, MAE P50 = 48.0; 08:00 open 16,600 → bands 16,685 / 16,515 (range P50), 16,652 (MFE P50), 16,552 (MAE P50). Session high 16,655 ≥ 16,652 → `mfe_p50_hit = 1`; high < 16,685 → `rng_p50_hit = 0`. SRP box 09:00–09:15 H 16,660 / L 16,640 (W 20): RE+1 = 16,680, RE−2 = 16,600. OHLC daily (18:00 anchor) open 16,650, high 16,700, low 16,630, close 16,690 (bull): manipulation = 16,650 − 16,630 = 20, distribution = 50.
- **Code.** **match** `family_env.py::build_env_table` computes `ev_*` (09:30 open ± mean/median 60-session AM excursion) and `ss_*` (09:00 open, one-sided means/medians); no nearest-rank session bands on the 08:00 NY Morning window, no 15-minute box RE ladder, no manipulation/distribution candle statistics.

### R-P17 — RTH 1m-OHLC volume profile with VA outcomes, and the 18:00 open · pass 0.6213 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[PINE Sessions & VP with prev session VP & daily weekly opens.txt:216–226]`: `body_top = max(c, o); body_bot = min(c, o); topwick = h − body_top; bottomwick = body_bot − l; body = body_top − body_bot; denom = 2·topwick + 2·bottomwick + body; bodyvol = body·v/denom; topwvol = 2·topwick·v/denom; botwvol = 2·bottomwick·v/denom`; `:231–232`: body volume goes to the green (up) or red (down) bucket by candle colour, wick volume split half/half. `:252–263` POC = the max-volume row's centre; `:265–289` VA = expand from the POC row alternately down then up until `volCnt ≥ volSum·vaWidPct/100` (70 default). `:641–642`: `pm6_sess = time(..., '1800-1801:23456')`, `weeklyO_sess = time(..., '1800-1801:1')` (Sunday 18:00 weekly open). `:84–90`: "6 PM Daily Open", "Weekly Open (6 PM Sunday)".
- **Procedure.** (1) Profile rows: `res` equal rows over [session L, session H] (the file's row count input; 1-tick rows named). (2) Per 1m bar allocate volume: body share = body/denom, each wick share = 2·wick/denom, spread uniformly over the rows the body / wick spans (`get_vol`); body volume to up/down by colour; wick volume half to each. (3) POC = centre of the max-volume row; VA = 70% expanded from the POC one row at a time on the heavier side (the file alternates below then above per iteration). (4) Session = prior RTH (09:30–16:00); levels VAL/POC/VAH fixed at 16:00. (5) `lvl.1800open` = the 1m open at 18:00 (Sun–Thu), `lvl.weekly.open` = Sunday 18:00 open. (6) Outcomes: touch / reject / hold at VAH, VAL, POC, 18:00 open, weekly open in the next RTH.
- **Constants.** Printed: 70%; 18:00; Sunday. Source-unspecified: row count.
- **Different object if.** The trade profile (`value.vp.rth.trade`) is used (different construction, 1-tick bins from prints), or the profile is 06:00–09:00.
- **Fixture.** Bar: o 100.0, h 101.0, l 99.0, c 100.5, v 100 → body 0.5, topwick 0.5, bottomwick 1.0, denom = 1 + 2 + 0.5 = 3.5 → bodyvol 14.29 (up bucket), topwvol 28.57 (14.29 up / 14.29 down), botwvol 57.14 (28.57 / 28.57). Rows of 0.25: the body spans 100.0–100.5 (2 rows → 7.14 each), the bottom wick 99.0–100.0 (4 rows → 14.29 each). 18:00 open 2024-01-02 = the first 1m open at 18:00 ET (value from `cov.nq.ohlc1m`; `sessions_F.open` = 16,657.75 is the 06:00 box open, not this level).
- **Code.** **match** `family_open.py::ohlc_vp` builds a body/wick profile for the open cell (`VAL_ohlc/VAH_ohlc`) but scores no VA touch/reject outcomes; no 18:00 / Sunday-open level object (`clocks.py` has no `lvl.1800open`).

### R-P19 — Body gap ≥ 4 ticks, near-edge fill · pass 0.0386 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[PINE 8020 System.txt:50]`: `gapMinTk = input.int(4, "Min gap size (ticks)")`. `:309–318`: `leftTop = max(open[1], close[1]); leftBot = min(open[1], close[1]); rightTop = max(open, close); rightBot = min(open, close); minGap = gapMinTk·tick; if rightBot − leftTop ≥ minGap → newGap(rightBot, leftTop, true) // up gap: [leftTop .. rightBot]; else if leftBot − rightTop ≥ minGap → newGap(leftBot, rightTop, false)`. `:251–253`: `if not g.filled and (g.isUp ? low <= g.top : high >= g.bot) → g.filled := true` (fill = touch of the near edge). `:259–267` repairs: a candle with no wick on one side (`lowerWick <= tol` for an up candle → marker at the low). `:120–126`: 80/20 levels = prices ending in 20 and 80 of each 100-point block; `:45` confluence within 8 ticks.
- **Procedure.** (1) 1m bars (the chart timeframe; 5m named). (2) Up gap between consecutive bars when the current body bottom − prior body top ≥ 4 ticks (1.0 pt); zone = [prior body top, current body bottom]; down mirror. (3) Fill = a later bar's low ≤ zone top (up gap) / high ≥ zone bottom (down gap): the near edge. (4) Rows: fill rate by 16:00; time to fill; confluence flag if a zone edge lies within 8 ticks of an 80/20 level; repairs (no-wick candles) as level markers with tap rate.
- **Constants.** Printed: 4 ticks; 8 ticks; 80/20 grid; repair wick tolerance 0 ticks. Source-unspecified: none.
- **Different object if.** The gap is the three-bar wick FVG (R-P11), or the fill is measured to the far edge.
- **Fixture.** Bars: prior o 100.00 / c 100.50 (body top 100.50); current o 101.75 / c 102.25 (body bottom 101.75) → 101.75 − 100.50 = 1.25 ≥ 1.0 ✓ → up gap [100.50, 101.75]. Later low 101.5 ≤ 101.75 → `filled = 1` (near edge; the far edge 100.50 not required). 80/20 check: 101.75 vs nearest level 120 → 18.25 pts > 2 pts → `conf = 0`.
- **Code.** **match** `family_gap.py::_body_gap` flags the presence of an adjacent body gap on the 09:00 hour (no ≥ 4-tick threshold stated as the Pine input, no near-edge fill outcome, no 80/20 confluence).

### R-P20 — MVFL delta zones and volume-anomaly zones · pass 0.6569 (rescored 2026-09-10, n 647)

- **Source.** `[MVFL L5]`: `htf = input.timeframe("5")`; `L9–10`: 4 of 7 votes to flip bias; `L19–23`: `ltf = "1"` sub-bars, `vdMult = 6.0`, `vdAvgLen = 50`, `vdFixed = 3000`; `L30–35`: `kClusters = 4`, `minZonePct = 45`, `kmIter = 12`, `zonePadAtr = 0.4` (× ATR-14); `L40–45`: `vaLength = 20`, `vaFactor = 2.5`, `vaGrosorPorc = 0.2` (% of price), `vaZonaUmbral = 0.5` (% merge); `L54–56`: London 02:00–08:00, NY 08:00–16:00 ET session filter. `L80–81`: `ltfArr = request.security_lower_tf(..., "1", close > open ? volume : close < open ? -volume : 0.0); barDelta = sum`; `L83–86`: `avgAbsDelta = sma(|barDelta|, 50); effThresh = max(avgAbsDelta·6, 3000); sigEvent = |barDelta| > effThresh`. `L101–102`: `volAnomAvg = sma(volume, 20); isVolumeAnomaly = volume > volAnomAvg·2.5`; `L113–136`: a new anomaly within 0.5 % of an existing centre replaces it only if its volume is larger; `L171–175`: `mitad = (c * (vaGrosorPorc / 100.0)) / 2` — the box spans ± half the 0.2 % thickness. `L144–148` / `L426–430`: bullish if `(close > L and close[1] <= L) or (close > L and low <= L and low[1] > L)` (breakout or rejection upward), bearish mirror.
- **Procedure.** (1) 5m bars in the session filter (02:00–08:00, 08:00–16:00 ET). (2) Bar delta = Σ over the bar's 1m sub-bars of (+V if close > open, −V if close < open); signal when |Δ| > max(6 × SMA50(|Δ|), 3000); the bar's price = its close (the file clusters signal prices). (3) Delta zones: k-means (k = 4, 12 iterations) on signal prices per side (bull / bear), zone = [cluster min, cluster max] padded to ≥ 0.4 × ATR-14, hidden if the cluster weight < 45% of the strongest; edges = the zone bounds. (4) Volume-anomaly zones: bars with volume > 2.5 × SMA20(volume); zone = close ± 0.1% of price (`vaGrosorPorc` = 0.2 % is the full thickness: `mitad = (c × 0.2 % ) / 2`, `L171–175`); merge if within 0.5% of an existing zone (keep the larger volume). (5) Per zone edge L: bullish vote = (close > L and prior close ≤ L) or (close > L and low ≤ L and prior low > L); bearish mirror. (6) Outcomes: after a vote, G-default reject/hold at L; agreement of the 4-of-7 bias with the next 5m direction (label). Aggressor-side delta (`value.delta.rth.trade`) is a named variant, tape-gated.
- **Constants.** Printed: all above. Source-unspecified: none.
- **Different object if.** The delta is aggressor-side (a different, gated object), or zones are drawn on 1m bars without the session filter.
- **Fixture.** 5m bar 10:15 with sub-bar deltas +900, +1,200, −300, +1,500, +800 → Δ = +4,100; SMA50(|Δ|) = 550 → 6 × 550 = 3,300; threshold = max(3,300, 3,000) = 3,300 → 4,100 > 3,300 → `sigEvent = 1` (bull) at close 16,640. Volume anomaly: volume 9,000 vs SMA20 3,200 → 9,000 > 8,000 ✓ → zone [16,623.36, 16,656.64] (±0.1 %, full thickness 0.2 % = 33.28 pts). Vote at L = 16,640: 10:25 close 16,645 with prior close 16,638 ≤ 16,640 → `c4dir = +1`.
- **Code.** **match** (no MVFL objects; `_ohlc_cvd` is session-level).

## Part 2 — pass ids (33 at the first run)

Rates quoted are `RULES_SCORES.md` primary rates on F (n = 647) for the scorer predicate named in the block. "match" means the predicate computes the source procedure; the named departures are recorded where the predicate computes something narrower or different.

### R-J01 — Judas reversal, trade #2 (fade at exhaustion, 09:40–09:50) · pass 0.0185 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[TBR p.8]`: "Here is where we participate in the reversal during the 9:40 - 9:50 window, levels of interest will be the mean reversal levels or -0.5 projection." `[TBR p.5]`: "Mean reversal levels toggling / projections toggling / Area shading of mean reversal". `[TBR p.30]`: "the overall percentage of reversals happening at the -0.5 projection of the range (86.46%). This analysis was done on 4537 days of data … we can see how significant are also the 'mean reversal levels' projections, holding a very high percentage of reversal across all the days analysed … All data statistics are based on the 9:00am - 12:00pm time window"; the same page's console prints "Extended Range: ±0.5 STDV / Reversal Percentage from Extended Range = 86.46% / Average Reversal Time (Original Range) = 09:47:36 / (Extended Range) = 09:51:05", and its bar chart "Reversal % by Projection (Upper vs Lower)" prints the depths 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 / 0.5 with lower ≈ 87 / 82 / 78 / 70 and upper ≈ 85 / 80 / 74 / 66 (read from the archived page render). Charts: `[TBR p.9]` Trade #2 reverses from above the high (sweep into the +0.5 side inside the 09:40–09:50 shade, then down through EQ to −0.5 / −1); `[TBR p.10]` Trade #1 pokes above the high first, then runs below the low through −0.5 (wick beyond it) inside the shade, and Trade #2 reverses from there to +1.66 / +2; `[TBR p.13]` Scenario #1 reverses between the high and +0.5 on the mean-reversal lines; `[TBR p.20]` shades the reversal area between the high and +0.5 with the three dotted mean-reversal lines inside it. `[XF p.47]`: "86.46% of reversal of off the -0.5 stdv (exhaustion)". `[TBR p.20]`: "constant rejection and multiple wicks". `[TBR p.12]`: "i only look to enter positions after open." `[FIND p.4]`: "Sweep extends to exhaustion (−0.5 / mean-reversal / P-zone), then reverses through mid toward the untouched edge."
- **Procedure.** (1) Box 06:00–09:00 from 1s bars: H, L, W. Exhaustion ladder on both sides: `lvl.mr.0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3` = H + k·W above / L − k·W below, and ±0.5 = H + 0.5·W / L − 0.5·W; `area.mr` = edge → ±0.5 (the p.5 "area shading of mean reversal"). (2) After 09:30 the first excursion beyond an edge (`b.wick`) defines the swept side, either side; depth = the maximum excursion beyond that edge in W units; the first touch (`t2`, 1m bars) of each ladder level is timed and binned (09:30–09:40 / 09:40–09:50 / 09:50–10:00 / 10:00–10:30 / 10:30–12:00). (3) Reject = G-default at the deepest ladder level touched (1m close ≥ 0.5·W back toward the box within 15 min, no `b.c1` beyond ±0.5 + δ first); R = W. The manual prints no reversal definition, so the return-inside reading is printed beside it: `rev.inside` = a 1m close back inside [L, H] by 12:00 after the excursion (the "false breakout" of `[TBR p.6]`). (4) Overshoot: a ladder level counts as touched-and-held when the excursion beyond it is ≤ δ, δ ∈ {2 ticks, 0.1·W} (`proj.overshoot.δ`; the wick through −0.5 on p.10 and through +0.5 on p.20 is drawn, the tolerance is not printed). (5) Draw rows: EQ reach, clean-edge reach (the edge not swept), leftover Asia / London extreme (R-J10), by 10:00 / 12:00. (6) The 86.46% claim in two readings, upper and lower side separately and pooled: (a) share of 09:00–12:00 reversals (first turn of ≥ 0.25·W, named) whose excursion reached ±0.5 (the text's "at the −0.5 projection"); (b) share of edge sweeps that reversed (`rev.inside`) without exceeding ±0.5 (the console's "from Extended Range"); plus the per-depth reject rate at 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 / 0.5 per side (the p.30 bar chart recomputed) and the two average reversal times (original range = first return inside [L, H]; extended range = first return inside [L − 0.5·W, H + 0.5·W]). A location share, not P(win) (`[FIND p.12]`).
- **Constants.** Printed: 09:40–09:50; ±0.5; the depths 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 / 0.5 (p.30 chart axis); 86.46% (4,537 days, 09:00–12:00); 09:47:36 / 09:51:05. Source-unspecified: the reject depth and window (grid defaults), the touch tolerance, the overshoot δ, the reversal definition behind the 86.46% and the p.30 bars.
- **Different object if.** The touch is a close-through (the manual's Judas is a false breakout), or the window is 09:30–10:00 without the location (a plain reversal-time histogram), or the sweep stops at the 6–9 edge without entering the mean-reversal area (a 6–9 H/L tag is not the exhaustion location), or only the low side or only the ±0.5 line is scored (the charts draw the +0.5 side and the 0.1–0.3 lines), or the levels are measured in EV width (no source does).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Width base `w.69` (faithful) | `w.ev` (EV band width; only the unexplained chart label "EVrange −60%" `[PACK L24]` hints at it) | `w.london` | `w.own` (the R-J23 clock's own box). Level ±0.5 | ±1.33 | ±1.66 | band 1.33–1.66 | overshoot δ past the printed level — each on both sides, one slot at a time (RULES C3, SL-width and SL-proj).
- **Fixture.** Session 2024-03-13: H 18,238.0, L 18,165.0, W 73.0 → low side 0.1 = 18,157.7, 0.2 = 18,150.4, 0.3 = 18,143.1, −0.5 = 18,128.5; high side 0.1 = 18,245.3, 0.2 = 18,252.6, 0.3 = 18,259.9, +0.5 = 18,274.5; δ = 0.1·W = 7.3 → a low of 18,121.2 still counts as a −0.5 touch. Retained: `m05_bin` = bin.0940-0950, `m05_reject` = False → `j01 = 0` (no 0.5·W close-back within 15 min). Synthetic low side: −0.5 = 80.0 touched 09:44 (low 79.75), close 90.25 at 09:55 (≥ 80 + 10 ✓) → `reject = 1`. Synthetic high side (H 110, W 20 → +0.5 = 120.0): high 120.25 at 09:46, close 109.5 at 09:58 (≤ 120 − 10 ✓) → `reject = 1`, side = high.
- **Code.** **match** `sessions.py::projections()` prints `m05_high` / `m05_low`; `family_levels.py::build_level_table` scores both with `grid.py::outcomes_at_level` and takes the earlier touch as `first_side`; `m05_in_0940` is now `m05_bin == bin.0940-0950 and m05_reject`, so `recipe_score.py::_preds.j01` is reject-only (the earlier window-touch OR is gone; rate 0.0185). Not computed: `lvl.mr.0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3`, `area.mr`, the overshoot tolerance (touch is `t2` only), `rev.inside`, the per-side depth table, the original-vs-extended reversal times, any EV-width level; the clean-edge and leftover draws stay outside the predicate.

### R-J02 — Judas rider, trade #1 (09:30 open into the projections) · pass 0.2813 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[TBR p.8]`: "the first trade is where we position ourselves right at the open where we participate in the judas move (false breakout) towards the projections of the range where the reversal will take place. The time window of this trade spans from 9:30 open until the 9:40 -9:50 reversal window … I suggest to have positions closed as we reach the reversal window, the yield of this trade outcome will vary depending on the size of the 6-9 range (e.g. the distances from open to projection levels)". `[TBR p.5]`: "Projection direction plotting" (formula unpublished). Charts: `[TBR p.10]` Trade #1 pokes above the high into the +0.5 side first, then runs below the low through −0.5 with the wick beyond it inside the 09:40–09:50 shade; `[TBR p.9]` Trade #1 runs below the low into the low-side mean-reversal lines and the reversal leg climbs to the +0.5 side — "the projections" are the mean-reversal ladder and ±0.5 on whichever side the open leg runs.
- **Procedure.** (1) 09:30 open = the 09:30 1m open. (2) Success = a touch (`t2`) before 09:40 of ±0.5 on either side (both sides printed; the side of the first edge taken after 09:30 and the side nearer the open are named rows); printed per depth as well — first touch of `lvl.mr.0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3` and ±0.5 before 09:40 — and with the overshoot reading (excursion ≤ δ beyond the level). (3) Rows: share of sessions; excursion from the open to 09:40 in points and in R = |open − (±0.5 of that side)|; Model A filter (R-J06) as the condition.
- **Constants.** Printed: 09:30; 09:40–09:50. Source-unspecified: the side rule; which depth counts as "the projections" (all printed).
- **Different object if.** The touch is counted up to 09:50 (that overlaps trade #2), or the target is the 6–9 edge, or only one side is scored, or the levels are measured in EV width (no source does).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Width base `w.69` (faithful) | `w.ev` | `w.london` | `w.own`; level ±0.5 | ±1.33 | ±1.66 | band 1.33–1.66 | overshoot δ — each side (RULES C3).
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: open 16,610.0; W 67.75; −0.5 low 16,564.125, +0.5 high 16,699.625; the first ±0.5 touch printed at 10:17 (bin.1000-1030, low side) → `open_to_m05_before_0940 = 0`. Synthetic low side: open 100, −0.5 low 90.0; low 89.9 at 09:37 → `= 1`, excursion 10.1 pts = 1.01 R. Synthetic high side: H 120, W 20 → +0.5 = 130.0; high 130.1 at 09:35 → `= 1`, side = high.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.j02` = `open_to_m05_before_0940` (`family_levels.py`: the 09:30–09:40 window's high ≥ `m05_high` or low ≤ `m05_low`, either side; rate 0.2813). The per-depth rows (0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3), the overshoot reading and the excursion-in-R rows are not computed.

### R-J03 — Single break, extended overnight range (scenario 1) · pass 0.0000 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[TBR p.12]`: "Scenario #1 (Extended overnight range): In the case of having an extended overnight range, the EQ or quadrants become our levels on interest. This is usually where i lower my expectations during AM session, the 6-9 range high/low are sufficient as target for the most part, the projections are off the table and after 10am all interest in being in a position is not longer present. Price tends to consolidate all the way through lunch/PM session." `[TBR p.24]`: "Extended Overnight Session (6am -9pm) usually present on 8:30 red folder news — Targets in these conditions will be limited to the high and low of the overnight range until lunch time."
- **Procedure.** (1) Extended = `w.rel-prior-rth ≥ 1.0` (W69 / prior RTH range; the ≥ 1.0 is the closed default, not printed) or an 08:30 red-folder day. (2) Single break = path class high-only / low-only on 1m closes 09:30–12:00 (`b.c1` beyond one edge, never the other). (3) Retrace = after the break, a touch `t2` of EQ (Q25/Q75/OP as named rows) and a hold `h=15` in the break direction (1m closes stay on the break side of EQ for 15 min). (4) In before 10:00: the retrace must occur by 10:00. (5) Reach of the broken edge by 10:00 / 12:00; consolidation share through lunch (12:00–13:00 range ≤ 0.5·W, named).
- **Constants.** Printed: 6–9; 10:00; 09:40–09:50 close. Source-unspecified: the extended threshold, hold window.
- **Different object if.** The retrace has no hold (a touch of EQ after any break), or the 10:00 cutoff is dropped.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: `w_rel_prior_rth` 0.270 (< 1.0) and not a red-folder day → `extended = 0` → not eligible. Synthetic: W69 80, prior RTH 70 → 1.14 ≥ 1.0 ✓; closes > H from 09:41; EQ touch 09:52 (low EQ + 0.25) and closes ≥ EQ to 10:07 → `single_ext_retrace = 1`, in before 10:00 ✓.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.j03` = `extended and path_class in (high-only, low-only) and midretrace`; `sessions.py::_midretrace` = a later touch of EQ after the break with no hold and no 10:00 cutoff; `extended` uses `w_rel_prior_rth ≥ 1.0` or `red_folder_0830` (the named default). Hold `h=15`, the 10:00 cutoff, and the edge-reach rows are missing. Rescored 2026-09-10 (`RULES_SCORES.md` event: "single-break on extended overnight, EQ retrace with h=15 by 10:00"; `_preds.j03` now uses `midretrace_hold_1000`; rate 0.0000): the hold and the 10:00 cutoff named above were added to the retained code; not re-audited line by line in this pass.

### R-J04 — Single break, overnight already purged (scenario 2) · pass 0.0294 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[TBR p.12]`: "Scenario #2 (Stop Hunts - relative strength/weakness between indices during overnight): When overnight presents stop hunts/ swing failure scenarios and every significant overnight liquidity has been purged, During AM session price usually don't present a fake breakout to knock people out of positions, we can anticipate price expanding from the inner range levels (EQ, Quadrants). In this case scenario my targets expectation increases, we can notice a shrank overnight range leading to explosive expansions during the AM session discarding the 9:40 9:50 window to be of any interest for reversals but rather use it as continuation where i would look to add to my position." `[TBR p.21]`: 1.33/1.66. `[XF p.48]`: "1.33 1.66 end of day max expansion".
- **Procedure.** (1) Purged = every significant overnight liquidity taken before 09:30: Asia H and L and London H and L each swept (1s high ≥ / low ≤) by 09:30 — the manual's "every significant overnight liquidity" (the code's rule, |H69 − Asia/London H| ≤ 2 ticks or the low mirror, is a different reading: the 6–9 box itself printed at the overnight extreme). Shrunk overnight = `w.rel-prior-rth ≤ 0.5` (closed default). (2) Single break as R-J03. (3) Expansion from the inner level: `b.c1` beyond EQ / quadrant in the break direction after 09:30. (4) Reach of 1.0 (H + W / L − W), 1.33, 1.66 by 10:30 / 12:00 / 16:00; 09:40–09:50 continuation = no reversal ≥ 0.25·W inside the window; re-entry count after a knock-out (a `b.c1` against the break then a new hold) on expansive days.
- **Constants.** Printed: EQ, quadrants; 1.33/1.66. Source-unspecified: purged rule, shrunk threshold.
- **Different object if.** Purged is read as "6–9 H/L equals the overnight extreme" (the code) instead of "the overnight extremes were swept before 09:30".
- **Fixture.** 2025-09-15: `purged = 1` (code rule), path high-only, `ext100_reach = 1` → `j04 = 1`; source rule: Asia H 24,131 / L 24,092, London H 24,150 / L 24,120.25 vs 6–9 H 24,174.25 / L 24,092.25 — the 6–9 box exceeded the Asia/London highs (swept) and touched the Asia low within 1 tick; both sides taken → `purged_source = 1` as well. 2024-03-13: code `purged = 1`, `ext100_reach` (1.0 reach) not shown; predicate as scored.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.j04` = `purged and single-side path and ext100_reach`; `sessions.py::_purged` tests the 6–9 extreme against the Asia/London extremes (≤ 2 ticks), not a pre-09:30 sweep of all overnight liquidity; no 1.33/1.66 by horizon, no 09:40–09:50 continuation share, no re-entry count. Rescored 2026-09-10 (`RULES_SCORES.md` event: "overnight Asia and London both swept by 09:30, single-break reach of 1.0"; `_preds.j04` now uses `purged_source`; rate 0.0294): the source reading of purged replaced the box-touches-extreme rule; not re-audited line by line in this pass.

### R-J05 — Single break, range-open and mid retrace (2026 use) · pass 0.1855 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[XF p.12]`: "done for the day at open / single break behaviour through A period, range mid provided the entry area / +124 points". `[XF p.15]`: "two retracement-to-mid stats at play from the lows, 6-9 range and 15m OR / but nothing beats being done in the first 20 mins of market open". `[XF p.23]`: "Retrace to midpoint stays at 60.4%". `[XF p.25]`: "don't really use ORs that much, i sometime use the 5m OR for reference or the 15m". `[FIND p.4]`: "Price breaks, returns to range open (OP), continues in the break direction. Open is an entry magnet, not a fade. Mid of the range is the other common entry."
- **Procedure.** (1) A-period single break: `b.c1` beyond one 6–9 edge in 09:30–10:00 with the other edge clean. (2) Retrace: touch `t2` of EQ (60.4% claim) and, as separate rows, of OP (range open = the 06:00 first print) and of the 15m OR mid ((H+L)/2 of 09:30–09:45). (3) Hold = G-default reject in the break direction at the retraced level (R = W). (4) Reach after the hold: the broken edge / 1.0 by 12:00; the first-20-minute exit row = broken edge reached by 09:50.
- **Constants.** Printed: 60.4%; 15m / 5m OR. Source-unspecified: hold rule.
- **Different object if.** Any-side path with a later EQ touch (no break-then-return order), or the retrace is measured on the 09:30–12:00 window without the A-period condition.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: path low-only, `midretrace = 1` → `j05 = 1`; EQ 16,631.875 touched after the low break (`eq_touch = 1`). OP = 16,657.75 (06:00 open) — `op_touch_after_break` row exists in `level_grid_F`. Claim: 60.4% to recompute by width class.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.j05` = single-side path and `midretrace` (EQ only; OP and OR-mid retraces live in `op_touch_after_break` / `or15_return_low` but are not in the predicate); no hold at the level, no A-period restriction (the path class is 09:30–12:00). Rescored 2026-09-10 (`RULES_SCORES.md` event: "single-break midretrace to EQ / range open"; rate 0.1855); not re-audited in this pass.

(4) Rows: path class shares per cell (double = both edges `b.c1`); outside-both + RVOL ≥ 1.0 (09:30–09:35 volume / 60-session median) → double-break rate (the "discard mean reversion" cell); `open.oneway.A` = 09:30–10:00 stays one-way (no return to the OR low) — the 76% claim.

- **Code.** **match** `_preds.j06` = `outside_both and rvol_ge_1 and path_class == "both"` (the double-break rate in the discard cell; the source predicts it low). `family_open.py::build_open_table` provides the cell, RVOL, `oneway_A`, `or5/or15_return_low`.
