### R-J08 — 1.33 / 1.66 retracement-reversal fork (AM into PM) · pass 0.0216 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[TBR p.21]`: "It's important to note the 1.33 1.66 levels and monitor the interaction at these levels. They function as Retracement/Reversal area where price will continue the direction of AM or reverse all of the AM session through PM session." Its figure shades a band from −1.33 to −1.66 below the low: in the left panel the "Retracement" box sits on the band (price holds inside it, then continues down to −2 … −4), in the right panel the "Reversal" box sits on it with the wick poking through −1.66 before the session reverses up through the box to +0.5. `[TBR p.10]` Trade #2 prints +1.33 / +1.66 / +2 above the high. `[TBR p.36]`: "AM Consolidation → PM Expansion … Target: Higher projections from the AM session range; AM Expansion → PM Consolidation … PM often consolidates between key AM session levels" (AM 09:30–12:00, Lunch 12:00–13:00, PM 13:00–16:00). `[XF p.31]`: "price keeps reacting from the 1.33 1.66 area / it's not random, just math" (chart: the band shaded below the low, the reversal wick into it). `[XF p.33]`: "a reversal from the 1.33 1.66 area" (same drawing). `[XF p.48]`: "1.33 1.66 end of day max expansion" (chart: the band shaded above the high with the RTH high inside it). `[PACK L66]`: chart labels "−133% / −166% under the low".
- **Procedure.** (1) Levels on both sides: 1.33 = H + 1.33·W above / L − 1.33·W below, 1.66 likewise; `band.133-166` = the closed interval between them on each side. The coordinate is his chart's (`[PACK L66]`, `[TBR p.10, p.21]` labels), the same family as ±0.5 ("half the range projected beyond the broken side" `[FIND p.16]`) and ±1. `[FIND p.16]`'s fib list read literally on a 0→1 tool would put 1.33 only 0.33·W beyond the edge; that is the named alternative `env.ext.133.from-origin`, not drawn on any Jumbo chart. No Pine file is used for this geometry. (2) Touch `t2` in 13:00–16:00 (12:00–16:00 named) of 1.33, of the band interior, or of 1.66, on the side the AM leg ran; the depth reached (1.33 / inside / 1.66 / beyond 1.66 by ≤ δ / beyond) is a printed column. (3) Reject = G-default at the deepest level touched (R = W, k = 15, no `b.c5` beyond 1.66 + δ first); continue = `b.c1` beyond 1.66 + δ with `h=30`; δ ∈ {2 ticks, 0.1·W} (`proj.overshoot.δ`, the p.21 reversal wick). (4) AM class: compressed AM = 09:30–12:00 range ≤ 0.5·W (named); PM reach of the band on those sessions; end-of-day max expansion = the RTH high (or low) inside the band on that side.
- **Constants.** Printed: the names 1.33 / 1.66 `[TBR p.21]`; the chart labels ±1.33 / ±1.66 `[TBR p.10, p.21]` `[PACK L66]`; the AM / Lunch / PM windows `[TBR p.36]`. Source-unspecified: the compressed-AM threshold, the overshoot δ; the anchoring is read from the chart labels, not from a printed formula.
- **Different object if.** 1.33 is measured from the origin (L + 1.33·W = H + 0.33·W), or the touch is in the AM (that is `ext133_am`), or the box is the London box (that is R-J09's London row), or only the low side is scored (XF p.48 and TBR p.10 draw the band above the high), or the band is reduced to its two lines (the source shades the area), or the width is EV (no source does).
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Width base `w.69` (faithful) | `w.london` | `w.own` | `w.ev`; level ±0.5 | ±1.33 | ±1.66 | band 1.33–1.66 | overshoot δ — each side (RULES C3).
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: H 16,665.75, L 16,598.0, W 67.75 → above: 1.33 = 16,755.8575 (retained `ext133_h` ✓), 1.66 = 16,778.215, band [16,755.8575, 16,778.215]; below: 1.33 = 16,507.8925 (retained `ext133_l` ✓), 1.66 = 16,485.535, band [16,485.535, 16,507.8925]; δ = 0.1·W = 6.775. `ext133_pm_reject = 0` on 2024-01-03 and 2025-09-15 (no PM touch-and-reject). Synthetic above (H 110, W 20): 1.33 = 136.6, 1.66 = 143.2; 13:40 high 140.0 (inside the band), 13:52 close 126.0 ≤ 136.6 − 10 ✓ → `band_pm_reject = 1`; a 13:41 high of 144.5 (1.3 beyond 1.66, ≤ δ = 2.0) still counts as a band touch with overshoot.

Not computed: the band as an area (reject from inside it), the overshoot tolerance, the continue-vs-reject split at 1.66, the compressed-AM condition, the end-of-day max-expansion share, any London-width or EV-width row.

### R-J11 — SessionStat 9–12 as the target map and confluence · pass 0.7496 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[SS p.6]`: "Average and Median Levels: It calculates the average and median of the Highs and Lows over the lookback period chosen. Minimum average Levels: It computes additional levels based on the minimum average of the session, which reflects price movements of shorter swings. Projections: Optional custom upper and lower projections are calculated by extending the average range (difference between upper and lower levels)." `[SS p.10]`: "9:00 AM–12:00 PM: New York AM Session (High and Low)". `[SS p.11]`: "if the historical average expansion is 10 points, but today's range is only 2 points, the high level at open + 10 is unreachable." `[XF p.19]`: "range exhaustion levels and 9-12 statistical session boundaries both meeting up nicely". `[XF p.45]`: "It's the minimum average range if the 9-12 session". `[FIND p.13]`: "Why SessionStat default lookback is 60" is unpublished.
- **Procedure.** (1) Session 09:00–12:00; anchor = the 09:00 open (`[SS p.11]` "open + 10"). (2) Over the prior N = 60 sessions (named; the default is unexplained): up-excursion = H − open, down-excursion = open − L; levels = open + mean(up) / open − mean(down) (`avgHL60`), medians (`medHL60`), and minimum-average (`minavg60`: the source says "minimum average of the session … shorter swings" — the code's min(mean_up, mean_down) applied to both sides is a named reading; the source does not define it). (3) Projections = levels ± (upper − lower) (named as `ss.proj`). (4) Outcomes: reach (`t2`) of the upper or lower level by 12:00; overshoot; G-default reject at the level; reject at the coincidence with −0.5 / 1.33 (within `tR`) vs either alone.
- **Constants.** Printed: 09:00–12:00; simple / weighted average, median. Source-unspecified: N = 60 (unexplained), the minimum-average formula, the anchor being the 09:00 open (inferred from p.11).
- **Different object if.** The anchor is 09:30 (that is `env.ev.*`), or the range (H − L) is averaged instead of the one-sided excursions.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: `ss_mid` 16,617.0 (09:00 open), `ss_hi` 16,641.75, `ss_lo` 16,449.0 → up-mean 24.75, down-mean 168.0 (asymmetric means over the prior 60); `ss_reach = 1` → `j11 = 1`. 2025-09-15: 24,172.75 / 24,257.97 / 24,072.78; `ss_reach = 1`.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.j11` = `ss_reach` (`family_env.py::build_env_table`: one-sided means from the 09:00 open over 60 sessions, AM reach by 12:00). `minavg` = min(mean_up, mean_dn) is the code's reading of an undefined term (the wiki names it differently; logged). The coincidence-with-−0.5 row is not computed.

### R-J12 — P-zone at the range edge with the range open · pass 0.0015 (rescored 2026-09-10)

- **Source.** `[XF p.29]`: "one and done on the early fade of the range, highest probability of the time segment. / - P-zone / - low > range open / Full reversal during 9:40-9:50". `[XF p.28]`: "10 am p-zone > london low". `[XF p.30]`: "P-Zone is just the name i gave it. it's a number of things that define areas of 'support/resistance' based on distances that are filtered by volume and volatility. each ticker has its own zone". `[FIND p.8]`: "Anchor a session (6–9, London into 03:00, RTH, 18:00). Measure distance bands from open and from mid that kept reacting over the last N similar-vol sessions … A lone P-zone in air, with no TBR level and no node, is not his trade. On 2 Jan 2026 the fade was P-zone and 6–9 low above range open." `[FIND p.4]`: exhaustion = "−0.5 / mean-reversal / P-zone" on the swept side.
- **Procedure.** (1) `pz.approx.A` (wiki approximation; the auto-learn weights are unpublished): anchors 09:00 / 09:30 / 10:00; over the prior 500 sessions (named), the distribution of the AM excursion from the anchor; band = [p50, p75] (named) of that excursion on each side, kept only on similar-vol sessions (named) → `pz_hi`, `pz_lo`, T1 = p50 reach. (2) Overlap on either edge: long case — the band contains the 6–9 L or the mean-reversal area just beyond it, and the session low so far > the 6–9 range open (OP); short case — the band contains the 6–9 H or the area beyond it, and the session high so far < OP (the mirror; the 2 Jan 2026 example is the long case and the sources print no short example, so the mirror is the same object on the other side, not a new rule). (3) Event: the R-J01 sequence at the band — first touch of the band in 09:40–09:50, then G-default reject from inside the band (not only at ±0.5; overshoot δ past the band's far edge allowed). (4) Rows: reversal-inside-band rate per side; "10 am p-zone > London low" = the 10:00 band lying above the London L and the London L reached after the reversal; distance ranking of EV / ±0.5-and-ladder / P-zone to the AM extreme, per side.
- **Constants.** Printed: none (the engine is unpublished). Source-unspecified: everything in step (1), the overshoot δ.
- **Different object if.** The band is used without a TBR level or node under it (`[FIND p.8]`), or the reversal is scored without the 09:40–09:50 window, or the reject is taken at ±0.5 instead of inside the band, or only the low side is scored.
- **Variants (named, not faithful).** Width base `w.69` | `w.ev` | `w.london` | `w.own` for the TBR level the band must overlap; level ±0.5 | ±1.33 | ±1.66 | band 1.33–1.66 | overshoot δ (the band may overlap any of them) — each side (RULES C3).

(2) Flag = body/range ≤ b (not printed; 0.3 named) and volume ≥ k × SMA14(volume) with a *trailing* 14-bar average (k not printed; 1.5/2.0/2.5 named). (3) Location: the candle's range within `tR` of a 6–9 level (H, L, EQ, Q25, Q75, −0.5). (4) Outcome: G-default reject at that level with vs without the candle; overlap with `flow.absorption.A`.

- **Source.** `[GB L28–29]`: "**9:00–10:00 AM ET.** 'I wait until after 10AM. The 9 to 10AM range is established. (4) Invalidation = the sweep wick (`[GB L212, L353]`); the pack adds that he reads invalidation on bodies, not wicks ("The bodies tell the story. The wicks do the damage" `[GB L92, L622]`), so a 1m / 5m close beyond the sweep extreme is the named body variant of IV-6; true breakout = `b.c1` + `h=30` outside. Both sides as stated ("Exact mirror" `[GB L216–227]`). (5) Outcomes: opposite-edge reach by 12:00 / 16:00; PDH/PDL confluence share (the swept edge within `tR` of PDH/PDL); agreement with Judas labels.
- **Constants.** Printed: 09:00–10:00; after 10:00; 5-minute close; illustrative ticket stops the pack reads off his screenshots and marks "not a formula — structure": ~32.5 pts (NYAM low-sweep long), ~34.5 pts (9–10 high short), ~49.75 pts above PDL, ~17 pts (Asia short above TDO) `[GB L356–358, L606]`. Source-unspecified: sweep depth, k.
- **Different object if.** The event is counted before 10:00 (`[GB L30]`: "he called that early"), or the fail is a 1m close (comparison row only), or the box is the 6–9 (Jumbo).
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: `fail_range.gb.nyam = 1`, `sweep_range.gb.nyam = 1` → `g01 = 1`. Synthetic: box H 110 / L 100; 10:12 high 110.75 (wick sweep), 10:20 5m close 109.5 (< 110 ✓, within 30 min) → `fail_nyam_c5 = 1`; low 100.25 at 11:40 → `opposite_edge_reach = 1`. Long mirror: 10:31 low 99.5 (wick sweep of L 100), 10:40 5m close 100.75 (> 100 ✓, within 30 min) → `fail_nyam_c5 = 1` on the low side; high 110.0 at 11:52 → `opposite_edge_reach = 1`.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.g01` = `fail_range.gb.nyam` (`family_fail.py::_failback` on `range.gb.nyam`: wick beyond either edge, then 5m close inside within k = 30; the sweep test is `h > H or l < L`, so the long mirror is in the predicate). Opposite-edge reach, PDH/PDL confluence and the body-variant invalidation rows are not in the predicate.

'Higher timeframe bullish, simple fib retracement, lower timeframe failed breakdown of that level, reclaim and enter long, stop below the level.'" `[GB L258–275]`: impulse → fib → pocket; bearish day → short location; "Clean rejection / failure to continue through the zone"; invalidation "above the zone for a short". `[GB L537–547]`: "~50% retrace into golden pocket, sweep above PDL, fail, short".
- **Procedure.** (1) Impulse = the NYAM move: on a bearish day from the NYAM (09:00–10:00, or the completed impulse's) high to its low; on a bullish day low to high; the HTF swing is the named alternative. (2) Pocket = [impulse end + 0.5·range, impulse end + 0.618·range] measured from the impulse end back toward its start (for a down impulse: L + 0.5·(H−L) to L + 0.618·(H−L)). (3) Direction: the pocket is a short location on a bearish day (retrace up into it) and a long location on a bullish day; the bias comes from R-G08. (4) Event: touch `t2` of the pocket, then G-default reject in the impulse direction (R = pocket height, k = 15) or a sweep + 5m close at a level inside it. (5) Outcome: reach of the impulse extreme by 16:00; traverse = `b.c1` through the far edge of the pocket.
- **Constants.** Printed: 50–61.8%. Source-unspecified: impulse definition beyond "the NYAM move".
- **Different object if.** The pocket is 38.2–50% (a different band), or it is measured on the 6–9 box (not his impulse), or the direction is ignored.
- **Fixture.** Down impulse H 110 → L 100: pocket = [105.0, 106.18]. 10:35 high 105.25 (touch), close 103.0 at 10:44 (≤ 105 − 0.5·1.18 = 104.41 ✓) → `gp_reject = 1`; low 100.0 at 12:10 → `impulse_extreme_reach = 1`. 2024-01-03: `gp_touch = 1`, `gp_reject = 1` under the code's band.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.g07` = `gp_touch`; committed: `family_levels.py::_gp_band` = [L + 0.382·W, L + 0.5·W] of the NYAM 09:00–10:00 box, direction-agnostic (`CONSTRUCTION_AUDIT.md`: "loc.gp | NYAM 0.382-0.5 band"); the source band is 50–61.8% of the impulse measured from its end, with direction. Working tree (uncommitted at the time of this pass): `formulas.py::gp_band_impulse(high, low, down)` = [L + 0.5·W, L + 0.618·W] for a down impulse and [H − 0.618·W, H − 0.5·W] for an up one, direction from the NYAM close vs open, reject side set by that direction — the source's band and direction; the impulse is still the 09:00–10:00 box's H / L rather than the completed impulse he measures, and the HTF-swing alternative is not built. Wiki page session-fail-boxes states 50–61.8 correctly.

### R-G08 — Overnight PDL / PDH sweep-and-reclaim bias · pass 0.1685

- **Source.** `[GB L313]`: "A reclaim of PDL overnight is, in his words, 'one of the cleanest Green Bird entry models' and can **create the next day's bias** (get long overnight, buy pullbacks in NYAM)." `[GB L443–450]`: "If overnight already did the clean reclaim (e.g. PDL swept and held): Bias is set. During NYAM he **buys pullbacks** into discount / golden pocket rather than only fading a fresh range." `[GB L543–544]`: "Bias from *overnight* PDL sweep and reclaim. Long overnight. NYAM: buy pullbacks / golden pockets / sweeps in the direction of that reclaim."
- **Procedure.** (1) PDL/PDH = prior RTH 09:30–16:00 1s L/H. (2) Overnight (18:00–09:30): sweep `b.wick` below PDL then reclaim = 5m close back above PDL (GB confirmation) and hold on the original side to 09:30 (no 5m close back below; named). (3) Bias = long (mirror for PDH). (4) Outcomes: next-morning path class (09:30–12:00) conditional on the bias; NYAM pullback-to-discount reach (touch of the golden pocket of the 09:30→10:00 impulse) then reject.
- **Constants.** Printed: overnight; PDL. Source-unspecified: hold-to-open rule.
- **Different object if.** The reclaim is a 1m close (the code), or the sweep is of the Asia edge (R-G02).
- **Fixture.** 2024-03-13: `prior_rth_overnight_reclaim = 1` → `g08 = 1` (PDL 17,930 swept overnight and reclaimed). 2024-01-03: 0 (open 16,610 stayed below PDL 16,622.5).
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.g08` = `prior_rth_overnight_reclaim` (`family_levels.py`: overnight 18:00–09:30 high > PDH + 2 ticks (or low < PDL − 2 ticks) and the overnight window's last 1m close (09:29) back inside PDH/PDL — the reclaim is read from the window's final close, not from a 5m close after the sweep, and no hold-to-open rule is applied). Path-class-conditional and pullback rows are not in the predicate.

Rate 0 on F: no Monday session satisfied stack + fill.

### R-A10 — Open-type / day-type gate · pass 0.4096

- **Source.** `[AMT1 p.11]`: "OPEN DRIVE: Price drives hard in one direction straight off the open and never trades back through it … OPEN TEST DRIVE: The market opens, tests a key reference first, prior low, prior value edge, finds no business there, then drives the other way … OPEN REJECTION REVERSE: Opens, auctions one way, gets rejected and trades back through the open … OPEN AUCTION: Price rotates around the open with no conviction either way." `[AMT1 p.10]`: "TREND DAY: Opens near one extreme, closes near the other … NORMAL DAY: A wide early range, then rotation inside it … NORMAL VARIATION: The early range gets extended once, roughly doubling it … NEUTRAL DAY: Range extension on both sides of the open … NON TREND DAY: Nobody shows up. Narrow, quiet". `[MAMT p.18]`: Steidlmayer's six types "based on how the first hour's range compared to what followed it". `[MAMT p.20]`: "Trend Day … price discovery extends beyond IBx2 … Neutral Extreme … exceeds both the IBH and IBL and settles near an extreme … Neutral Day … breaching IBH and IBL but finding fair value within the IB range … Normal Day … IB containment".
- **Procedure.** Open label from the first 30 minutes (09:30–10:00): *drive* = the 30-minute range never trades back through the 09:30 open (all 1m lows > open for an up drive; mirror); *test-drive* = a touch `t2` of a key reference (prior VAL/VAH, PDL/PDH) within the first 30 minutes followed by a drive away that does not trade back through the open after the test; *rejection-reverse* = price auctions one way then a 1m close back through the open; *auction* = otherwise. Day label from the completed RTH: trend (open and close in opposite 10% bands of the RTH range, named), normal (IB not extended), normal-variation (one-side extension ≥ 1× IB, named "roughly doubling"), neutral (extension both sides), non-trend (RTH range ≤ 0.5 × 20-session median, named). Rows: open × day transition matrix; fade vs continuation outcome by label; agreement with `day_type`. Second label set, kept separate: `label.amt.day.mamt4` from `[MAMT p.20]` — *trend* = the RTH range extends beyond 2 × the IB range in one direction ("price discovery extends beyond IBx2", printed); *neutral extreme* = both IBH and IBL breached and the close in the outer band of the RTH range ("settles near an extreme"; the band is unprinted → outer 20% named); *neutral* = both breached, close inside the IB; *normal* = neither breached ("IB containment"); the p.20 ES shares (trend 20.95–29.25%, neutral extreme 9.49–17.79%, neutral 9.09–12.25%, normal 22.13–31.62%) and the p.23 IB rows are the recompute claims.
- **Constants.** Printed: first 30 minutes; "roughly doubling"; IBx2 (MAMT). Source-unspecified: the numeric thresholds above.
- **Different object if.** Drive is defined vs the prior VA instead of vs the open (the code), or the day label is read before 16:00.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: open 16,610 < VAL 16,685 and the 30-minute high < VAL → code `amt_open_label = drive`; source: drive requires no trade back through 16,610 in 09:30–10:00 (`or5_return_low = 0`, `or15_return_low = 1` → a return to the OR low happened, but the open itself: not stored) — condition stated. 2024-03-13: `auction`. 2025-09-15: `drive` (open above VAH, low stays above VAH).
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.a10` = `amt_open_label == "drive"`; at the first run `family_gap.py::_amt_open_label` labelled drive against the prior VA, and `RULES_SCORES.md` (2026-09-10, rate 0.2179) records the event as "first 30m never trades back through the 09:30 open", the source's definition — not re-audited line by line here; no `label.amt.day.mamt4`. `_amt_day_label` maps path class to trend/non-trend and uses close-in-value + a 0.25 symmetry rule for neutral/normal (named thresholds).

- **Source.** `[MAMT p.15]`: "There is a 94% chance of price touching either the overnight high or the overnight low during the current session." `[MAMT p.16]`: "If the RTH session opens inside the previous ETH profile's balance, there is a 73% chance of the session going on to hit that ETH profile's MPOC, its mid." `[MAMT p.21]`: "Overnight Session Levels, touched during the current RTH session: ONH & ONL Touched 20.16 / 23.75 / 22.22 / 23.85; ONH or ONL Touched 92.49 / 95.40 / 94.64 / 95.00; ONH 60.47…; ONL 52.17…; ONVAH 75.10…; ONVAL 70.75…; ONVPOC 90.12 / 86.21 / 87.74 / 90.00" (ES, 1,040 days). `[MAMT p.22–23]`: pClose / pHOD / pIBH / pIBL / pLOD / pOpen / pVAH / pVAL / pVPOC touch tables by open location; 1/2 gap.

(2) Touch = 1m high ≥ / low ≤ during RTH 09:30–16:00 (the retained AM 09:30–12:00 window is a named narrower row). (3) Open-location split (vs prior RTH close / HOD / LOD / IB / VA). (4) Rows: the p.21–23 tables recomputed on NQ.
- **Constants.** Printed: the tables; 94%; 73%. Source-unspecified: the half-gap anchor for opens inside the prior range (no row), the ETH-profile window beyond what the drawings label.
- **Different object if.** The touch window is the AM only (the code), or MPOC is the volume POC, or the "ETH profile" is taken as 18:00–16:00 (the drawings bound it by the overnight high and low), or the half gap is anchored on the prior close for a session gap.

### R-R02 — VIX regime gate · pass 0.3910

- **Source.** `[VIX4 p.3]`: "It measures implied volatility on S&P 500 options, essentially what traders expect the market to move over the next 30 days"; its figure prints "Total Range estimate calculation: Expected Daily Move (%) = VIX / √252". `[VIX4 p.4]`: "VIX around 12.5: ES roughly a 30 point day … VIX around 16: ES roughly a 50 point day. The sweet spot for structured R setups … VIX around 22: ES roughly a 95 point day … Ranges above are typical, the tool does the maths for the actual number." `[VIX4 p.5]`: "VIX above 20: Widen stops … VIX 15 to 18: The best pocket for pressing structured risk-reward … VIX below 14: Shrink targets to scalps … VIX below 13: Expected ES range under 30 points … RANGE COMPLETION: Track realized range against the implied range through the day." `[VIX4 p.6]`: "ES up, VIX down: Healthy trend … ES up, VIX up: Divergence … ES down, VIX up: Normal risk-off … ES flat, VIX flat: Expect chop … Rising VIX intraday, expect ES to push away from value, expansion. Falling VIX intraday, expect ES to rotate and respect levels, balance." `[VIX4 p.7]`: pre-event rise, post-event crush. `[VIX4 p.8]`: "CONTANGO … BACKWARDATION: Spot VIX spikes above the longer dates … VVIX". `[VIX4 p.9]`: "Balance on the profile, VIX dropping: Play the rotations … VIX rising: Prepare for the range break."
- **Procedure.** (1) VIX level known before the open = the prior session's VIX close (FRED VIXCLS dated the prior day); bands from the printed cut points — < 13 (no edge), 13–14 (scalps), 15–18 (sweet spot), > 20 (widen); 14–15 and 18–20 are named filler bins (the lesson prints no rule for them); the 12.5 / 16 / 22 anchors are "typical" ES ranges. (2) Implied daily range = price × VIX / √252 / 100 (the p.3 formula), applied to NQ at the NQ price (the lesson's numbers are ES points; the S&P-based VIX is the printed input; VXN — the Cboe Nasdaq-100 index, in the inventory `[INV L610]` — is the named twin `vol.vxn`). (3) Rows: realized RTH range by band; realized range / implied range (range completion, FL-15) by band; prior-VA edge-reject rate by band; fade vs break shares by VIX direction (today's close vs prior close — daily, known at 16:00, a label row; the four intraday combos of p.6 need intraday VIX, not in the inventory); balance-day rotation share split by VIX falling vs rising (p.9); contango / backwardation from the VX curve (`[INV L566–569]`) and VVIX (`[INV L610]`) as named slicers, unscored until joined.
- **Constants.** Printed: √252; 12.5 / 16 / 22 (ES 30 / 50 / 95 pts); cut points 13 / 14 / 15–18 / 20. Source-unspecified: which VIX print (the lesson says "read the number before the open"; the prior close is the pre-open value), the NQ application.
- **Different object if.** The same-day VIX close is used as the pre-open regime (a lookahead: the close is known at 16:15), or VIX / 16 replaces VIX / √252 (that is the Pine object, R-P16), or the bins are scored as if the lesson printed 13–15 and 18–20 rules.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: prior close VIXCLS(2024-01-02) = 13.20 → band 13–14 (scalps); implied daily move = 13.20 / √252 = 0.8315 % → at NQ 16,610 ≈ 138.1 pts; the same-day close 14.04 is not the pre-open value. 2025-09-15: the earlier fixture's 15.69 was the same-day close; the pre-open value is VIXCLS(2025-09-12) (not retained here); `r02 = 1` iff it lies in [15, 18).
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.r02` = `vix_band == "15-18"` with `formulas.py::vix_preopen` taking the prior session's VIXCLS (`RULES_SCORES.md` event "prior-session VIXCLS close in band 15-18"; fixture `R-R02.prior_close`); `formulas.py::vix_band` bins at 13 / 15 / 18 / 20 and omits the printed 14 cut. Realized-range-by-band, the implied range (VIX / √252) and completion rows, the VIX-direction split and the VX-curve slicer are not computed.

### R-P08 — IB path class (Pine IB statistical mapping) · pass 0.7991

- **Source.** `[PINE Initial Balance Statistical Mapping.txt:10–11]`: `ib_session = "0930-1030"`, `rth_session = "0930-1600"`. `:44–52`: `EXT_HIGH_25TH = 0.140; _MEDIAN = 0.314; _75TH = 0.601; _90TH = 0.997; EXT_LOW_25TH = 0.163; _MEDIAN = 0.380; _75TH = 0.794; _90TH = 1.366` (% of price beyond the IB edge). `:55–59`: `[sample_size, break_high_pct, break_low_pct, neither_pct, midpoint_retest_pct, median_retest_mins, bullish_close_pct]`, e.g. `BULL_LOW_ABOVE = [1281, 80.2, 17.0, 2.8, 76.6, 10.0, 76.7]`, `BEAR_HIGH_BELOW = [1127, 19.3, 73.9, 6.7, 81.0, 6.0, 28.0]`. `:68–75`: break-count arrays.
- **Procedure.** (1) IB = 09:30–10:30 H/L/open/close; combo key = (IB candle colour bull/bear, first extreme touched high/low, open above/below the IB midpoint — the array names). (2) After 10:30 to 16:00: break high / break low / neither (wick beyond; the Pine reads `high > ib_high`), midpoint retest = after price has first left the IB mid by more than 0.1 % of the mid (`threshold = ib_mid_price * 0.001`, `:279–283`), a later bar whose high or low comes back within 0.1 % of the mid (`:284–286`) — a leave-and-return band of roughly 17–25 NQ points, not a line touch; median minutes to retest, bullish close (16:00 close > 09:30 open). (3) Extension targets = IBH × (1 + p%) / IBL × (1 − p%) at the four percentiles; reach rows.
- **Constants.** Printed: all above, including the 0.1 % leave-and-return tolerance of the midpoint retest. Source-unspecified: none (the combo key follows the array names; the exact "ABOVE/BELOW" reference is the IB midpoint by the file's `ib_mid_price` usage at `:279`).
- **Different object if.** The break is a 1m close (the retained `ib_single` uses `b.c1`), or the window ends at 12:00.
- **Fixture.** IB H 16,660 / L 16,640 (bull, open 16,645 < mid 16,650 → "BELOW"); first touch after 10:30 = low → key BULL_LOW_BELOW `[141, 42.6, 54.6, 2.8, 96.5, 1.0, 64.5]`; median extension target below = 16,640 × (1 − 0.0038) = 16,576.8; midpoint-retest band = 16,650 ± 16.65 (a 10:41 low of 16,660 after a 10:35 close of 16,671 → left; an 11:02 low of 16,662 → back within 16.65 → `mid_retest = 1`). Retained 2024-01-03: `ib_single = 1` → `p08 = 1`.
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.p08` = the R-A15 predicate (single / both / neither by `b.c1`); the combo key, the 0.1 % midpoint-retest band, and the extension percentiles are not computed (`RULES_SCORES.md`: "break combo reduced").

### R-P13 — Midnight-open (TDO) traded through in the NY window · pass 0.5858

- **Source.** `[PINE NQ Hourly Retracement Levels.txt:128]`: `is_sesa = math.sign(nz(time(tf, "0800-1600", tz)))` (NY tracking window 08:00–16:00). `:279–297`: `"12:00am_open" => 73.67; "12:00am_midpoint" => 74.09; "07:00am_midpoint" => 94.19; "02:00am_midpoint" => 76.68` (overall hit rates). `[PINE nq_stats_mapper:310–313]`: `hit_7to8_mid = 94.19; hit_midnight = 73.75; hit_2to3_mid = 76.68` (by NY position and session pattern). `[GB L24–26]`: TDO = the 00:00 print.
- **Procedure.** (1) Level = the 00:00 ET 1m open (the Pine "12:00am open"); also the 00:00–01:00 hour midpoint and the 07:00–08:00 / 02:00–03:00 hour midpoints. (2) Hit = any 1m bar spanning the level (high ≥ level ≥ low) during the NY window 08:00–16:00 (the Pine window); the retained 09:30–12:00 row is narrower. (3) Split by NY open above/below the London midpoint and by the Asia/London pattern.
- **Constants.** Printed: 08:00–16:00; the rates. Source-unspecified: none.
- **Different object if.** The window is 09:30–12:00 (the retained `tdo_touch`, a different denominator), or the level is the 18:00 open.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: TDO 16,695.0; AM range 16,598–≈16,650 → `tdo_touch = 0` (AM); the 08:00–09:30 segment (6–9 box H 16,665.75) also < 16,695 → the 08:00–16:00 hit needs a PM print ≥ 16,695 (not retained). Claim: 73.75% (08:00–16:00).
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.p13` = `tdo_touch` (`family_fail.py`: touch in 09:30–12:00); the Pine window is 08:00–16:00 (`RULES_SCORES.md`: "P13 09:30–12:00 vs Pine 08:00–16:00").

### R-P16 — Expected-volatility band (VIX/16) · pass 0.7713

- **Source.** `[PINE Expected Volatility .txt:11–13]`: `sess_Time = "1800-1600"`, `vol_symbol = "NASDAQ:VOLI"`. `:30–50`: `close_day = close[1]; Index = request.security(..., "1D", close_day)` (prior daily settlement of the index and of the vol index). `:58–61`: `a = VolatilityIndex/16/100; b = VolatilityIndex/math.sqrt(365)/100`. `:67–100`: `logIndex = math.log(Index); Index_ZONE1UP_Res = exp(logIndex + a·1.0); Index_ZONE1DN_Res = exp(logIndex + b·1.0)` (zones at 0.25 / 0.5 / 1.0 / 1.5 with the a/b pair as the zone's two edges; supports mirror with b/a).
- **Procedure.** (1) Anchor = the prior daily settlement of the chart symbol (NQ daily close of the 18:00→17:00 session) and the prior daily close of the volatility index (VOLI = the Nasdaq-100 vol index; VIX is a substitute, named — the inventory has VIXCLS only). (2) a = V/16/100, b = V/√365/100 (b < a). (3) Zones in log space: upper zone k = [anchor·e^(k·b), anchor·e^(k·a)] for k ∈ {0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5}; lower zone k = [anchor·e^(−k·a), anchor·e^(−k·b)]. (4) Session = 18:00→16:00; outcome = touch of each zone's inner/outer edge; the "inside ±1 SD" claim (`[PINE NQ Stats Price Distributions.txt:302]`: 75.2% close inside ±1 SD on the daily anchor) as the calibration row.
- **Constants.** Printed: 16; √365; zone multipliers; 1800-1600; prior settlement anchor. Source-unspecified: VIX vs VOLI (VOLI absent; VXN, the Cboe Nasdaq-100 volatility index in the inventory `[INV L610]`, is the nearer named substitute than VIX; the VIX4 lesson's own divisor is √252, R-R02).
- **Different object if.** The anchor is the 09:30 open with the same-day VIX (the retained construction), or the band is linear (open × V/16/100) rather than log-space with the a/b pair.
- **Fixture.** Prior settle 16,600, V = 14.04 → a = 0.008775, b = 0.007349; upper 1.0 zone = [16,600·e^0.007349, 16,600·e^0.008775] = [16,722.4, 16,746.3]; lower 1.0 zone = [16,455.0, 16,478.5]; 0.25 upper = [16,630.5, 16,636.4]. Retained 2024-01-03: `ev_vix16_inside = 1` (AM within 16,610 ± 16,610·14.04/16/100 = ±145.7 → [16,464.3, 16,755.7]).
- **Code.** **match** `_preds.p16` = `ev_vix16_inside` (`family_levels.py`: 09:30 open ± open·VIX/16/100, AM 09:30–12:00 high/low inside, same-day VIXCLS); the Pine anchors at the prior settlement, uses log-space a/b zones over 18:00–16:00 and the prior-day vol close (`RULES_SCORES.md`: "P16 anchor/window/log-space/VIX vintage differ").


---

## Part 3 — shared sourced objects the recipes need (no R- id of their own)

Each object below is used by several Part 1–2 blocks. Jumbo objects cite Jumbo materials only (§0.1); code paths are the retained implementation.

### P3-01 — `range.6-9.published`: the 6–9 box, internals, projections

- **Source.** `[TBR p.7]`: "06:00am - 09:00am (ONS session - main New York model)". `[TBR p.8]`: "Time range selection: 6:00am - 9:00am". `[TBR p.4]` (indicator settings): "Custom range time selection / Custom quadrant levels 25% 75% / Equilibrium / Range open and close levels / Custom High and Low lines / Table showing range size in points". `[TBR p.5]`: "Mean reversal levels toggling / projections toggling / Area shading of mean reversal / Projection direction plotting / Retracement/reversal levels". `[TBR p.8]`: "levels of interest will be the mean reversal levels or -0.5 projection". `[XF p.47]`: "the coordinates are based on the 06:00 - 09:00 range and its levels / An astonishing 86.46% of reversal of off the -0.5 stdv (exhaustion)". `[FIND p.16]`: "−0.5: Half the range projected beyond the broken side / Range open: First print of the build window". `[FIND p.7]`: "1.0 projection … 1.33–1.66 band". `[PACK L66]`: chart labels "−133% / −166% under the low". `[TBR p.6]`: "all times … Eastern Standard Time, ET". Chart labels `[TBR p.9–10, p.13, p.20–21]`: +0.5 / +1 / +1.33 / +1.66 / +2 above the high and −0.5 / −1 / −1.33 / −1.66 / −2 (−2.5, −3) below the low, three dotted mean-reversal lines between each edge and its ±0.5, the reversal area shaded between the high and +0.5 (p.20) and the 1.33–1.66 band shaded below the low (p.21); `[TBR p.30]` bar chart depth axis 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 / 0.5, upper and lower side.
- **Procedure.** (1) Window 06:00:00–08:59:59 ET on NQ 1-second bars (`cov.nq.ohlc1s`; 1-minute fallback), NY trade date; H = max high, L = min low, W = H − L; range open = the 06:00 first print, range close = the 08:59 last print. (2) EQ = L + 0.5·W; Q25 = L + 0.25·W; Q75 = L + 0.75·W. (3) Projections beyond each edge, always on both sides and always in units of W: the mean-reversal ladder `lvl.mr.0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3` = H + k·W / L − k·W with the shaded `area.mr` from the edge to ±0.5; ±0.5 = H + 0.5·W / L − 0.5·W; ±1.0 = H + W / L − W; 1.33 = H + 1.33·W / L − 1.33·W and 1.66 likewise with `band.133-166` between them; ±2.0 = H + 2·W / L − 2·W (−2.5 / −3 also drawn) (R-J08 for the coordinate evidence; TBR p.9–10, p.13, p.20–21 for the drawings). A touch of any level carries the overshoot reading `proj.overshoot.δ`, δ ∈ {2 ticks, 0.1·W} (named). Width base `w.69`; `w.london` / `w.own` for the other clocks; `w.ev` is a named test variant only (no source states an EV-width projection). (4) `known_at` = 09:00; outcome windows 09:30–12:00 (default), 09:30–10:30, 09:30–16:00 (named). (5) Width tables: `w.pct.0859close` = 100·W/close(08:59); `w.pct.0930open` = 100·W/open(09:30); `w.rel-prior-rth` = W / (prior RTH H − L); bins [0,0.3), [0.3,0.5), [0.5,0.8), [0.8,1.2), [1.2,∞).
- **Constants.** Printed: 06:00–09:00; 25% / 75%; ±0.5; the depths 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3; ±1; 1.33 / 1.66 (names and chart labels); ±2; 86.46%; 4,537 days. Source-unspecified: the overshoot δ, the 1-second bar source (the indicator runs on the chart timeframe), the width-% denominator (08:59 close vs 09:30 open; both printed as rows).
- **Different object if.** The box is any other clock (`range.5-9`, `range.7-9`, `range.8-9` — "almost any time window between 5am and 9am will behave similarly" `[XF p.7]` — are comparison rows, not the published box), or the projections are measured from the origin, or the ladder is drawn on one side only, or a level is measured in EV width (no source does; `w.ev` is a named variant).
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03 (`sessions_F`): H 16,665.75, L 16,598.0 → W 67.75; EQ 16,631.875 ✓; Q25 16,614.9375 ✓; Q75 16,648.8125 ✓; −0.5 low = 16,564.125; −0.5 high = 16,699.625; 1.0 high = 16,733.5; 1.33 high = 16,755.8575 (`env_F.ext133_h` ✓); 1.66 high = 16,778.215; 1.33 low = 16,507.8925; 1.66 low = 16,485.535; ±2.0 = 16,801.25 / 16,462.5; mean-reversal ladder low side 16,591.225 / 16,584.45 / 16,577.675, high side 16,672.525 / 16,679.3 / 16,686.075; δ = 0.1·W = 6.775; range open 16,657.75, range close 16,617.0; `w_pct_0859close` = 100 × 67.75 / 16,617.0 = 0.4077 ✓ → bin 0.3–0.5; `w_rel_prior_rth` = 67.75 / (16,873.5 − 16,622.5 = 251.0) = 0.2699 ✓.
- **Code.** **match for EQ / Q25 / Q75 / ±0.5 / ±1 / 1.33 / 1.66 on both sides; missing for the mean-reversal ladder, the two areas, ±2 and the width-base variants.** `sessions.py::build_session` (1s window via `clocks.py::clock_bounds`, `projections()` printing `m05_` / `ext100_` / `ext133_` / `ext166_{high,low}`, both width tables, `width_bin`); `known_at_ns` = window end. No `lvl.mr.*`, `area.mr`, `band.133-166`, ±2.0, overshoot reading or `w.*` variant exists.

### P3-02 — Path class, break order, day-type labels, reversal bins

- **Source.** `[XF p.23]`: "Range break classification filtered by range size … combined single break % (high-low) … Retrace to midpoint stays at 60.4%". `[XF p.17]`: "identifying range break behaviour (single/double)". `[TBR p.12]`: "Scenario #1 (Extended overnight range) … Scenario #2 (Stop Hunts …) When overnight presents stop hunts/ swing failure scenarios and every significant overnight liquidity has been purged". `[TBR p.6]`: "'False Breakout' or judas swing … or just 'Range breakout'". `[TBR p.30]`: reversal-time histogram, 9:40–9:50 cluster, 09:00–12:00 window. `[FIND p.4]`: single break vs sweep-then-reverse cartoons; "decide which cartoon you are in before 09:30". `[JJX L56]` (user): "Classification of range (size, balance, already-purged overnight)".
- **Procedure.** (1) Break = first 1m close beyond an edge (`b.c1`) in the outcome window; class = high-only / low-only / both / neither; break order = which came first (ambiguous if the same bar). (2) Judas label (named rule; the manual draws it on both sides): after the first close beyond either edge, a later 1m close back through EQ = `judas.depth.any`; `judas.depth.mr` additionally requires the excursion to enter the mean-reversal area (≥ 0.1·W beyond the edge); `judas.depth.-0.5` requires it to reach ±0.5 on that side (overshoot δ allowed); the swept side is a column. (3) Extended = `w.rel-prior-rth ≥ 1.0` (closed default), compressed = ≤ 0.5. (4) Purged (manual: every significant overnight liquidity taken before 09:30): Asia H and L and London H and L each swept before 09:30 — the retained rule instead tests whether the 6–9 H or L sits within 2 ticks of an Asia/London extreme (R-J04). (5) Day type precedence: neither → judas → single-extended → single-purged → unmatched. (6) Reversal bins on the first −0.5 touch (`t2`): 0930-0950 (first 20 min) / 0940-0950 / 0950-1000 / 1000-1030 / 1030-1200 / other. (7) Mid-retrace = after the first break, a 1m close at or across EQ (no hold).
- **Constants.** Printed: the XF width bins and rates; 60.4%; ~45%; 09:40–09:50. Source-unspecified: the Judas close-back rule, the extended/compressed cut points, the purged rule.
- **Different object if.** Breaks are wicks (the XF table counts breaks, not sweeps — unpublished, so `b.c1` is the named reading), or the window is 09:30–10:30 (XF p.11's table window; a named row).
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: closes broke L (16,598.0) and never H → `path_class` low-only, `judas = 1` (a later close back through EQ 16,631.875 after the low break), `day_type` judas (judas precedes single-*), `midretrace = 1`, `reversal_bin` bin.1000-1030 (first −0.5 touch 10:17), `m05_side` low. 2025-09-15: high-only, `purged = 1` (6–9 L 24,092.25 within 1 tick of Asia L 24,092.0), `day_type` single-purged, `reversal_bin` bin.0930-0950.
- **Code.** **match** for the class/order/bins (`grid.py::path_class_from_closes`, `sessions.py::_reversal_times`, `reversal_bin`); **named departures**: `_judas_from_ohlc` (close-back through EQ; the manual prints no rule), `_purged` (box-touches-overnight-extreme reading), `_midretrace` (close crossing EQ with no hold), extended/compressed cut points (closed defaults).

### P3-03 — The shared grid (`G-default`): touch, wick, close-break, reject, hold, fail-back

- **Source.** Named confirmations, not invented: 5-minute close back through the level `[GB L89]`: "'I wait for the 5 min close back below the PDL after sweeping above it… once closed below, low risk entry'"; reclaim = "back through the level and hold on the original side" `[GB L88]`; constant rejection / multiple wicks at the projection `[TBR p.20]`; 3 attempts without follow-through = failed `[TBR p.37]`; raid ≥ 5 pts then close ≤ the range high within 120 min (R-P04, Pine, comparison). The tolerance `t2`, the depth 2 ticks, `r = 0.5`, `k = 15`, `h = 30` are grid choices (the wiki names the full grid `t0/t2/tR`, `r ∈ {0.25, 0.5}`, `k ∈ {5, 15, 30}`, `h ∈ {15, 30}`).
- **Procedure.** Level in ticks (0.25). *touch* `t2`: a 1m bar with low ≤ level + 2 ticks and high ≥ level − 2 ticks. *wick break*: high ≥ level + 2 ticks (resistance) / low ≤ level − 2 ticks (support). *close break* `b.c1`: 1m close beyond the level. *reject* (r = 0.5, k = 15): from the touch bar, walking forward ≤ 15 min: the first 1m close beyond the level → false; a 1m close ≥ r·R on the original side → true (R = the object's width; r_ticks = round(0.5·R / 0.25), min 1). *hold* (h = 30): after `b.c1`, every 1m close stays beyond the level for 30 min and the window has a bar in its last minute. *fail-back* (`b.c5`, k = 30): first wick beyond H or L (2-tick depth), then a 5-minute close strictly inside (L, H) with the 5m bucket start ≤ wick time + 30 min; 5m buckets are counted from the first bar of the window.
- **Constants.** Printed: 5-minute close (GB); 3 strikes (TBR); 5 pts / 120 min (Pine raid). Source-unspecified: every other grid cell.
- **Different object if.** The touch is a trade print at the level (`t0`), the reject is measured on wicks, or the hold allows a single close back inside.
- **Fixture.** `family_levels.py::level_fixtures`: resistance 100, W = 20 → r_ticks = 40 (10 pts); 1m bars (h/l/c): [100.5/99.0/99.75], [99.0/91.0/92.0], [92.0/89.0/90.0], [90.0/88.0/89.5] → touch at bar 1 (low 99.0 ≤ 100.5, high 100.5 ≥ 99.5 ✓), no close > 100, close 92.0 ≤ 100 − 10 = 90? ✗ at bar 2; bar 3 close 90.0 ≤ 90 ✓ → `reject = 1` within 15 min. Wick: high 100.5 = 100 + 2 ticks ✓ → `wick = 1`; `close_break = 0`.
- **Code.** **match** for the six events as named (`grid.py::outcomes_at_level`, `reject_after_touch`, `hold_after_break`, `failback_wick_c5`). **Inconsistency between implementations:** `family_fail.py::_failback` counts a sweep at any print beyond the edge (0-tick depth) while `grid.py::failback_wick_c5` requires 2 ticks; the two fail-back producers therefore differ at 1–2-tick pokes.

### P3-04 — Value areas and profiles (trade VP, OHLC VP, prior-day VA)

- **Source.** `[AMT1 p.5]`: "POC … The price where the most trading happened … VAH … The top boundary of the zone holding roughly 70% of the volume … On any fixed range volume profile, set Value Area Volume to 70." `[RTVP p.4]`: "the band where roughly 68 percent of the session's volume traded". `[C3 p.7]`: "Set the value area at 40% instead of 70% for intraday work." `[ABS p.7]`: "Previous day's VAH and VAL, more usable, because that day is closed." `[MAMT p.4]`: "Stop using VAH and VAL as levels … they are outputs that move every time volume prints." `[XF p.14]`: "Just RTH session VP and delta profile" (scope RTH).
- **Procedure.** (1) `value.vp.rth.trade`: NQ trade prints 09:30–16:00 (MBP-1 trade events or the trades schema), 1-tick bins; POC = max-volume bin (tie → the lower price); VA = expand from the POC one bin at a time toward the heavier neighbour (tie → up first) until ≥ 70% of total volume; VAL/VAH = the outer bins. Fixed at 16:00; used as the prior-day VA at the next open. (2) `value.vp.rth.ohlc1m`: per 1m bar, half the volume spread uniformly over [low, high] ticks and half over the body ticks; same VA expansion (a Pine-style proxy; the Pine file's body/wick weighting differs, R-P17). (3) Developing VA (intraday) = the same construction as-of each minute (moves; `[MAMT p.4]`). (4) 68% and 40% as named widths.
- **Constants.** Printed: 70%; 68%; 40%. Source-unspecified: bin size, the expansion tie rule.
- **Different object if.** The profile spans ETH (`[FIND p.8]`: "not a full ETH composite"), or the VA is a symmetric ±σ band.
- **Fixture.** Prior RTH 2024-01-02 (`prior_rth_trade_vp_F`): POC 16,700.0, VAL 16,685.0, VAH 16,779.75, 1,005 ticks, 496,978 lots. Synthetic bins (lots): 100.00:10, 100.25:30, 100.50:50, 100.75:40, 101.00:20 (total 150, target 105): POC 100.50 (50); neighbours 30 vs 40 → add 100.75 (90); next 30 vs 20 → add 100.25 (120 ≥ 105) → VA = [100.25, 100.75].
- **Code.** **match** for the construction (`family_open.py::value_area`, `ohlc_vp`, `scan_prior_rth_trade_vp`; `mbp1_objects.py::vp_rth`). **Departure:** the OHLC proxy's half-range/half-body split is the code's rule, not the Pine file's `2·wick/denom` weighting (R-P17).

### P3-05 — Open cell, RVOL, one-way A-period

- **Source.** `[XF p.8]`: "RTH open location in relation to previous day value/range and the current day 6-9". `[XF p.16]`: "RTH open inside previous day value/range, scalping territory". `[XF p.10]`: "discard mean reversion and range double breaks when these things align". `[PACK L31]`: "open outside prior value and prior range, with RVOL above average … about 76% of A-period (9:30-10:00) stays one-way and does not return to OR low, 15y sample. Recompute."
- **Procedure.** (1) At 09:30: open vs prior VA (below/in/above), vs prior RTH range (PDL–PDH), vs the 6–9 box → `open_cell` (27 cells). (2) RVOL = the 09:30–09:35 volume divided by the median of the prior 60 sessions' 09:30–09:35 volumes (needs ≥ 20 prior sessions; the wiki's named definition — "above average" is the tweet's phrase); flags ≥ 1.0 and ≥ 1.5. (3) `open.oneway.A.0930-1000` = the A-period never closes back across the 09:30 open against its first direction (named reading of "stays one-way"); `open.oneway.OR.5m/15m` = no return to the OR low after the OR is known. (4) Double-break rate per cell (R-J06).
- **Constants.** Printed: none (76% is a claim). Source-unspecified: RVOL window and baseline, the one-way rule.
- **Different object if.** "Outside" is measured vs the 6–9 only, or RVOL uses the full first 30 minutes.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: open 16,610.0 < VAL 16,685.0 (below), < PDL 16,622.5 (below), inside the 6–9 [16,598.0, 16,665.75] (in) → `below|below|in` ✓; `outside_both = 1`; `rvol_0930` null (missing first-5-minute volume) → `rvol_ge_1 = 0`; `oneway_A = 1`; `or15_return_low = 1`.
- **Code.** **match** (`family_open.py::build_open_table`: `loc_code` ×3, `rvol_0930`, `oneway_A`, `or5/or15_return_low`, `outside_both`, `in_value`); RVOL as coded: `rvol = first5.volume / median(rvol_hist[-61:-1])`, i.e. the prior 60 sessions' first-5-minute volumes, computed only when ≥ 21 sessions exist (the null on 2024-01-03 is the warm-up).

### P3-06 — Clocks, bars, coverage, `known_at`

- **Source.** `[TBR p.7]`: the eight published windows. `[TBR p.6]`: ET. `[FIND p.4–5]`: London box into 03:00, 06:00 handoff. `[GB L28–38]`: NYAM 09:00–10:00 (after 10:00), previous completed hour, Asia ≈ 20:00–00:00 (chart), London 02:00–05:00 (ICT clock, inferred). `[SS p.8]`: sessions are same-day timestamps; cross-midnight sessions need the 23:59 setting. Data: `data-coverage` (QuantPad 1s/1m UTC ms; MBP-1 UTC ns).
- **Procedure.** (1) Every clock = (build start, build end, outcome start, outcome end) in America/New_York with day offsets (Asia/overnight start on the prior calendar day). (2) Box from 1s bars over [start, end); `known_at` = end; outcomes from `outcome_start`; leakage flag = known_at > outcome_start. (3) Coverage: a session is dropped when the 1-minute occupancy of the box window or of the outcome window is missing > 10% of expected bars (`COVERAGE_MAX_MISSING`); the 1s series omits empty seconds, so coverage is judged on 1-minute occupancy. (4) Session key = NY trade date; session = 18:00 prior day → 17:00; early closes from the trading calendar.
- **Constants.** Printed: the windows. Source-unspecified: the 10% coverage rule (a project rule, `SPEC.md`).
- **Different object if.** Bars are volume-elapsed or dollar bars (named bar types), or a window is searched rather than declared.
- **Fixture.** `clocks_F` 2024-01-03 `range.5-9`: H 16,674.0, L 16,598.0, `n_box` 240 = `expected_box` 240 (1-minute occupancy of 05:00–09:00), `known_at_ns` = 1704290400000000000 = 2024-01-03 09:00:00 ET (14:00:00 UTC) ✓, `outcome_start_ns` = 09:30:00 ET ✓, `leakage = 0`. `sessions_F.missing_1s` 3,500 of 10,800 expected 1s bars on 2024-01-03 (empty seconds, not a coverage drop: `drop_coverage = False`).
- **Code.** **match** (`clocks.py::CLOCKS`, `clock_bounds`, `wall_ns`; `sessions.py::build_session` coverage).

### P3-07 — Green Bird levels: `lvl.tdo`, `lvl.nwog`, `lvl.0930open`, PDH/PDL, `loc.gp`, `label.aplus`

- **Source.** `[GB L24–26]` TDO = first print at 00:00 ET; `[GB L59–60, L68–69]` NWOG = Friday close vs Sunday 18:00 ET open, two lines; `[GB L30, L90]` 9:30 manipulation below the open then reclaim; `[GB L64]` PDH/PDL = previous day high/low; `[GB L73–78]` golden pocket 50–61.8% of the measured impulse; `[GB L93, L147]` A+ requires a sweep of the range being traded.
- **Procedure.** TDO = the 00:00 ET 1m open. NWOG = [Friday's last 1m close of the session (16:59 ET), Sunday 18:00 open]; endpoint variants named: the 15:59 RTH close (retained) and the 17:00 settlement. 09:30 open = the 09:30 1m open. PDH/PDL = prior session 09:30–16:00 1s H/L (early close respected). Golden pocket = [end + 0.5·range, end + 0.618·range] measured back from the impulse end (NYAM move by default). A+ = a sweep (`b.wick`) of the traded box occurred.
- **Constants.** Printed: 00:00; Sunday 18:00; 50–61.8%; 09:30. Source-unspecified: the "Friday close" print (16:59 vs 15:59 vs settle).
- **Different object if.** TDO is the 18:00 open (`lvl.1800open`, R-P17), the pocket is 38.2–50% (retained code), or A+ is keyed on the 6–9 (retained code).
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03: `tdo` 16,695.0 (00:00 open); `nwog_real = 1`, gap from Friday 2023-12-29 15:59 close to Sunday 18:00 open (values not stored in the table; `nwog_fill = 0` in the AM); PDH 16,873.5 / PDL 16,622.5; NYAM 09:00–10:00 box → code pocket [L + 0.382·W, L + 0.5·W]; `aplus = 1` (6–9 sweep).
- **Code.** `lvl.tdo` **match** (`family_fail.py` 00:00 1m open; `family_levels.py` `tdo_c5`); `lvl.nwog` **match on the object, named endpoint departure** (`family_fail.py`: Friday 15:59 1m bar close, Sunday 18:00 1m open; `friday = day − 3` so only Mondays are real; `nwog_fill` = AM prints inside the gap band); `lvl.0930open` **invented window** (09:30–09:45 cap); PDH/PDL **match** (`sessions.py::prior_rth_window`); `loc.gp` **mismatch** (`_gp_band` 38.2–50%, direction-agnostic); `label.aplus` **mismatch (box)** (`aplus_box = range.6-9.published`).

### P3-08 — MBP-1 tape objects (register)

- **Source.** `[XF p.25]` BigTrades 100 NY / 75 London; `[DOM6 p.3–7]` absorption / exhaustion; `[DOM7 p.3–5]` iceberg reload; `[FP8 p.5–6]` diagonal 3–4× imbalance, ≥ 3 stacked; `[REF p.5]` refill clusters; `[VWAP p.5]` CVD; `[CEX L129]` (user) SMT definition; `[XF p.14]` RTH VP + delta profile; `[JJX L294–346]` (user) absorption / pull / stack at the touch only; `[JJX L171]` iceberg inference is a weak flag; `[JJX L16]` MBP-1 only (no MBP-10 / MBO).
- **Procedure (as retained; the tape-trust status is `FINDINGS.md`'s).** Discovery cut 2024-01-02→2024-11-15 froze: print-size grid q50 = 1, q75 = 2, q90 = 3, q99 = 7 lots (n = 63,440,040 prints); 2-minute aggressive-volume q90 cuts buy 3,533 / sell 3,441 lots. `absorption_a`: 2-minute rolling aggressive volume toward the 6–9 H/L ≥ the frozen cut while price is within 2 ticks of the level, advance ≤ 2 ticks, then reversal ≥ 0.25·W69 within 15 min (variants w1m / w5m / q75). `absorption_b`: at the touch, consumed size ≥ q90 of touch sizes, BBO reload ≥ 50% within 500 ms, twice. `iceberg_touch_infer(k)`: a trade > k × displayed BBO then a reload at the same price. `footprint_4x`: AM-wide price ladder, 4× diagonal flags, 3 adjacent same-side flags anywhere. `on_touch_refill`: ≥ 3 same-side prints ≥ 100 lots within 2 min at ≤ 2 ticks, departure > 4 ticks, later return. `cvd_from_trades`: Σ(buy − sell) from 18:00 with size buckets ≥ 100 / 20–99 / < 20 (`part_big` = the ≥ 100 bucket). `smt_trade_nq`: NQ vs sister 1m OHLC on level set S1 (without PDH/PDL). `vp_rth`: same-session RTH 1-tick profile, POC / 70% VA / `dp_max` / `dp_min`. `tpo_trade_visited`: coverage flag. `bigtrade` = any NY print ≥ 100; `bigtrade_75ldn` = any 02:00–05:00 print ≥ 75.
- **Constants.** Printed: 100 / 75 lots; 3–4×; ≥ 3 stacked; 60/80/100-lot clusters (REF); 2 ticks (DOM7 "add on within 2 ticks"). Source-unspecified: the q90 cuts, 2-minute window, 0.25·W reversal, 500 ms, 50% reload, k.
- **Different object if.** Any of these is evaluated at a level other than where the recipe places it (most recipes need the object at a *prior-marked level*, while the retained functions run at the 6–9 H/L or session-wide).
- **Fixture.** 2025-09-15 (`mbp1_flow_F`): `n_trades` 90,372; `bigtrade = 1`, `bigtrade_75ldn = 0`, `absorption_A = 0` (all variants), `absorption_B = 1`, `iceberg_touch = 1`, `footprint_4x = 0`, `refill_ontouch = 0`, `cvd_trade` −3,938 (sign −1), `part_big` 172 (1 print ≥ 100), RTH VP POC 24,253.0 / VAL 24,242.25 / VAH 24,283.0, `dp_max` 24,248.0, `dp_min` 24,260.0.
- **Code.** `mbp1_objects.py` functions as listed; `footprint_stack3` and `smt_s1` are hard-set `True` in `_score_session` (no information); blocked objects per `FINDINGS.md` (CVD, SMT, absorption B, bigtrade q90) stay blocked.

### P3-09 — Statistical envelopes: `env.ev.*`, `env.ss.*`, `pz.approx.A`, `env.ext.*`

- **Source.** `[XF p.7]` "am vol expected range"; `[PACK L15–22]` EV range as a stretch band, estimator unpublished; `[SS p.6, p.10, p.11]` SessionStat averages / medians / minimum average / projections, 09:00–12:00, "open + 10"; `[XF p.30]` P-zone: "distances that are filtered by volume and volatility", formula unpublished; `[FIND p.8]` distance bands from open and mid over similar-vol sessions, snapped to VP nodes; `[XF p.34–39]` screenshot UI (500-session window, T1–T4) is appearance only.
- **Procedure (as retained).** EV: ref = 09:30 open; up/down AM excursions (09:30–12:00) of the prior sessions; band = ref + mean60(up) / ref − mean60(down); median60 variant; reach = AM high ≥ upper or low ≤ lower; calibration = both inside. SS: open = 09:00 1m open; excursions over 09:00–12:00; mean60 / median60 / minavg = min(mean up, mean down) on both sides; reach in 09:00–12:00. PZ: 500-session percentiles (50/75/90/95/99) of the same AM excursions from the 09:30 open; T1 = [p50, p75]; `pz_reach` = excursion ≥ p50 (warm-up ≥ 30 sessions). EXT: H + k·W / L − k·W on both sides, k ∈ {1.0, 1.33, 1.66}, AM reach; the band between 1.33 and 1.66, the mean-reversal ladder (0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3) and the overshoot reading are not built; the width base is W69 only (`w.london` / `w.own` / `w.ev` of RULES A1.2 are not built — and no source states an EV-width projection).
- **Constants.** Printed: 09:00–12:00 (SS); "60" (SS default lookback, unexplained); nothing for EV / PZ. Source-unspecified: EV estimator, PZ everything, minavg formula.
- **Different object if.** The anchor is the 09:00 open for EV (that is SS), or percentiles replace means (named rows).
- **Fixture.** 2025-09-15 (`env_F`): ref 24,177.75; `ev_hi` 24,257.133 (mean up 79.383), `ev_lo` 24,076.788 (mean down 100.963); `ss_mid` 24,172.75, `ss_hi` 24,257.967, `ss_lo` 24,072.779; `pz_hi` 24,252.5 (p50 up 74.75), `pz_lo` 24,102.0 (p50 down 75.75), `pz_t1_hi` 24,322.5 (p75 up 144.75), `pz_t4_hi/lo` per p99; `ext133_h` 24,283.31 = 24,174.25 + 1.33 × 82.0 ✓; `ext133_reach = 1`, `ev_reach_mean60 = 1`, `ss_reach = 1`, `pz_reach = 1`.
- **Code.** **match to the named constructions** (`family_env.py::build_env_table`; histories exclude the current session, no lookahead). The SS minimum-average is one of two readings of an undefined term (wiki patched to say so).

### P3-10 — Owed levels / unfinished business (`lvl.owed.nearest`)

- **Source.** `[VP2 p.6]`: "NAKED POC: A prior session's POC that price has not traded back to yet … A list of naked POCs above and below is a ready made target list." `[TPO p.5]`: single prints as unfinished business; `[TPO p.9]`: "Fresh single prints and poor extremes. These are the magnets, and your target list. Respect the finished business: Excess marks where an argument ended." `[TBR p.11]`: overnight session highs/lows "deleted once purged". `[FIND p.9]`: "Unfinished business is a target, not an entry by itself." `[C3 p.6]`: "Single prints from the TPO chart and balances … are the destinations price trades between." `[ANAT p.10]`: "Objective named. The higher timeframe level that is actually owed something."
- **Procedure.** At each 5-minute step: candidates = {naked prior-session POCs (R-A06 list), unfilled single prints and unrepaired poor extremes (R-A14), untested session extremes (Asia, London, prior RTH, overnight H/L)}; each carries its formation `known_at` and is retired at its first `t2` touch; `lvl.owed.nearest` = the nearest surviving candidate above and below with type and distance (ticks and W69 units). Outcome: reach by 12:00 / 16:00 by type.
- **Constants.** Printed: none. Source-unspecified: the step (5 min), the retirement tolerance (`t2`).
- **Different object if.** Retired candidates are kept (a plain level list), or the nearest is chosen by type priority instead of distance.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03 at 09:30 (price 16,610): candidates above = London H 16,716.75 (untested), Asia H 16,721.0, prior RTH H 16,873.5, naked POC 16,700.0 (prior RTH POC, untouched overnight if no print at 16,700 — the box high 16,665.75 < 16,700 ✓ untouched) → nearest above = 16,700.0 (naked POC, +90 pts = 1.33 W69); below = prior RTH L 16,622.5? already below the open (16,610 < 16,622.5) → it is above: distance +12.5; so nearest above = PDL 16,622.5 (type prior-RTH-low, already swept by the 09:30 open → retired if the open printed through it; the 06:00–09:30 low 16,598 < 16,622.5 → retired) → nearest above = naked POC 16,700.0; nearest below = none in the candidate set (Asia L 16,693.25 and London L 16,689.0 are above) → `below = unavailable`.
- **Code.** **missing** (no candidate ledger; the pieces exist as separate flags: `asia_high/low`, `london_high/low`, `prior_rth_high/low`, `prior_rth_trade_vp_F.poc`).

### P3-11 — Calendar and VIX inputs (`load_red_folder`, `load_vix`)

- **Source.** `[TBR p.22]`: "The three most important red folder news above [CPI, NFP, FOMC]"; `[TBR p.24]`: "Extended Overnight Session (6am -9pm) usually present on 8:30 red folder news"; `[TBR p.18]`: 10:00 releases delay cycle 2; `[VIX4 p.3]` figure: Expected Daily Move (%) = VIX / √252; `[VIX4 p.4–5]`: VIX anchors and cut points 13 / 14 / 15–18 / 20; `[VIX4 p.7]`: CPI / FOMC vol crush.
- **Procedure.** Red-folder sets from `free-sources/context__event-calendar__normalized`: `economic-releases.parquet` (`event_type` ∈ {cpi, nfp}, `event_time_et` 08:30; 424 rows) → `0830` dates; `fomc-meetings.parquet` (time None) → `fomc` dates; `1000` = ∅ (no 10:00 table in the inventory; BLS HTML calendars hold CPI and Employment Situation only). VIX = FRED VIXCLS by date (a same-day close; the pre-open value is the prior date's, which `formulas.py::vix_preopen` now takes); VXN / VIX3M / VVIX sit in the same FRED table `[INV L610]` as named twins.
- **Constants.** Printed: CPI / NFP / FOMC; 08:30; 10:00; VIX bands. Source-unspecified: none.
- **Different object if.** The 10:00 set is inferred from the 08:30 table (forbidden), or the VIX open replaces the close (not in the inventory).
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-05: NFP, `event_time_et` 08:30 → `red_folder_0830 = True` (64 of 647 F sessions); `release_1000 = False` for every session; 2024-01-03 `vix = 14.04` (VIXCLS dated 2024-01-03); the pre-open value = VIXCLS(2024-01-02).
- **Code.** **match** for the 08:30 / FOMC sets (`family_levels.py::load_red_folder`); **missing input** for 10:00 at the committed run; **match (vintage)** for the VIX gate since the 2026-09-10 rescoring (`formulas.py::vix_preopen`, fixture `R-R02.prior_close`), with `vix_band` bins at 13 / 15 / 18 / 20 omitting the printed 14 cut (R-R02).

### P3-12 — Options open-interest nodes (`value.node.oi.<product>.top3`)

- **Source.** `[GEX p.13]`: "CALL WALL: Largest call gamma above. PUT WALL: Largest put gamma below. MAX PAIN". `[GEX p.14]`: "Major strikes … High open interest … Front expiry & 0DTE". `[GEX p.16]`: "Gamma concentrates near the money". `[DRFL L12]` (user): "NDX open interest is usually accurate, but … open interest is [not] the best way to get proper levels because it's naive". `[CEX L565]` (user): "on NQ, the NDX levels feel more accurate to me … versus QQQ". `[DRFL L1096]` (user): cross-asset — SPX may hit its node and reverse while NQ has no level there. `[CEX L455–456]` (user): the gamma level on one index may fire while the sister's is weaker or unhit.
- **Procedure.** Per product (NDX, NDXP, SPX, SPXW; QQQ, SPY, NQ.OPT named): at 09:25 ET, from the ThetaData OPRA open-interest file of the exchange date (`OI_vintage`), contracts with dte ≤ 14; top 3 OI strikes above and below the product's own reference spot (index products: the prior daily cash close from `yahoo__cash-daily`; ETFs: the 1-minute close; NQ.OPT: NQ); distance in points and in ATR-14 of the product's daily series; tags `tagged` (spot series traded to the strike in the outcome window: ETF/NQ 1m high–low; index products daily cash high–low), `near.025` (|d| ≤ 0.25 ATR), `near.050`, else `far`. Mapped-to-NQ rows are experiments only; cash NDX/SPX minutes are not invented.
- **Constants.** Printed: 0DTE / front expiry (GEX); none numeric for OI ranking. Source-unspecified: top-3, dte ≤ 14, the near bands.
- **Different object if.** Gamma-weighted nodes (need IV; R-R01), or a strike mapped onto NQ used as the faithful row.
- **Fixture.** 2024-03-13 NDX (`options_F`): spot 18,219.109 (prior cash close), ATR 252.07 (from 80.89 pts = 0.3209 ATR); nodes above 18,300 (PUT, OI 956, dte 2, +80.89 pts = 0.321 ATR → `near.050`), 19,000 (CALL 407, 3.10 ATR, far), 19,200 (CALL 403, 3.89 ATR, far); below 17,750 (PUT 5,222, −1.86 ATR, far), 17,000 (PUT 2,899), 17,600 (CALL 2,416); `OI_vintage` 2024-03-13; `ndx_spot_source` "yahoo__cash-daily NDX prior close"; `cash_minutes_used = False`.
- **Code.** **match to the named construction** (`family_options.py::_top3_around`, `_near_tag`, `_atr14`, `_cash_ohlc`); no gamma, no flip (R-R01 missing).

### P3-13 — Volatility estimators (`vol.rv20`, `vol.gk20`, `vol.har`, terciles)

- **Source.** `[DTM L23]` (user): forward-vol model with GK / YZ / HAR-RV; `[JJX L147]` (user): regime better than VIX, using GK etc.; `[VIX4 p.4–5]` VIX bands as the tier-2 comparison. No source prints the estimator formulas; they are textbook definitions (named).
- **Procedure (as retained).** rv = √Σ(Δlog close)² over 1m RTH closes 09:30–16:00 (per session, not annualized); gk = 0.5·ln(H/L)² − (2 ln 2 − 1)·ln(C/O)² on the 18:00→17:00 session bar; rv20 / gk20 = mean of the last 20 values (≥ 5 to start); har = 0.5·rv_t + 0.3·mean(rv, last 5) + 0.2·mean(rv, last 22); terciles of rv20 frozen on F (cuts at the 1/3 and 2/3 quantiles).
- **Constants.** Printed: none. Source-unspecified: windows, weights (HAR's 1/5/22 with 0.5/0.3/0.2 are the code's).
- **Different object if.** The estimator is computed on the overnight only (the "vol as of 09:29" variant), which would be the pre-open-known version.
- **Fixture.** 2025-09-15 (`vol_F`): rv 0.003316, rv20 0.006549, gk 2.733e-05, gk20 7.005e-05, har 0.004267, `vol_tercile_rv` low; 2024-01-03: rv 0.008196, rv20 null (fewer than 5 sessions of history) → tercile null.
- **Code.** **match (prior sessions only) since the 2026-09-10 rescoring.** `family_vol.py::build_vol_table` now computes rv20 / gk20 / har from the histories *before* appending the current session (`rv20_excludes_self = True`), so `vol_tercile_rv` and `rv20_high` are known at 09:30; the first-run lookahead is recorded in Part 4 finding 2. The fixture values above predate the fix and were not re-read.

### P3-14 — Gap / block objects (`gap.fvg.first.clock`, `gap.body.adjacent`, `block.sweep.tbr.3m`, `cisd.fractal.literal`, `tpo.poor`)

- **Source.** `[TBR p.27–29]` three-candle OB / rejection block (R-J18); `[TBR p.35]` first presented FVG; `[PINE First presented FVG…:148–149]` (R-P11, tier 2); `[PINE 8020 System.txt:309–318]` body gaps (R-P19); `[OSF L1–118]` Open Source Fractal CISD (tier 2, R-P12); `[TPO p.7]` poor extremes (R-A14).
- **Procedure (as retained).** `_first_fvg_clock`: 5m bars of the 09:00 ET hour; a three-bar wick gap (bar3.low > bar1.high or bar3.high < bar1.low) → presence flag. `_body_gap`: consecutive 1m bodies not overlapping on the 09:00 hour → presence flag. `_sweep_tbr_3m`: 3m bars 09:30–10:30; a bar making a new series extreme with the next bar closing beyond it → flag (continuation, not a reversal OB at a level). `_cisd_closeback`: 15m close back through a swept level → flag. `_tpo_poor`: 1-point extreme row visited by exactly one 30m period.
- **Constants.** Printed: 5m (Pine FVG timeframe input), 4 ticks (Pine gap minimum, not applied by the code), 30-minute letters. Source-unspecified: the 09:00-hour scope, 3m for the sweep.
- **Different object if.** Each is tied to a level and given an outcome (the recipe rows above), instead of a session presence flag.
- **Fixture.** 2024-01-03 (`gap_block_tpo_F`): `fvg = 1` (`fvg_side` None), `body_gap = 1`, `sweep_3m = 1`, `cisd = 1`, `tpo_poor = 1`, `clock_hour` 9, `clock_tf` 5. 2025-09-15: `cisd = 0`, the rest 1.
- **Code.** **presence flags only** (`family_gap.py`); each recipe that needs them is marked mismatch/missing in Part 1–2 for the level tie and the outcome.

### 4.2 Construction findings that affect several ids

1. `value.kz` compares the AM extreme with the *same session's* full-RTH value area (`mbp1_objects.py::vp_rth(ev, am0, rth_end)`), unknown at the touch (R-A02, R-A16, R-F11).
2. `vol_tercile_rv` / `rv20` / `gk20` / `har` included the session being scored at the first run (`family_vol.py`), so every "by vol tercile" split of that run used a lookahead slicer (R-J11 calibration, P3-13); fixed in the working tree on 2026-09-10 (`rv20_excludes_self`), not re-audited beyond that flag.
3. `vix` / `vix_band` used the same-day VIXCLS close at the first run (R-R02, R-P16); the 2026-09-10 rescoring switched the R-R02 gate to the prior session's close (`formulas.py::vix_preopen`); R-P16 not re-checked; the bins still omit the lesson's printed 14 cut.
4. `absorption_candle` used a centred 14-bar volume average (7 future bars) and no level at the first run (R-J14); `RULES_SCORES.md` (2026-09-10) reports it rescored with a trailing SMA14 at a 6–9 level — not re-audited here.
5. `footprint_stack3` and `smt_s1` are constants (`True`) in the MBP-1 scorer (R-F04, R-R04).
6. `range.gb.hour` steps every 5 minutes (31 boxes per AM instead of one per completed hour), which is why R-G03 saturates (0.9985).
7. Two fail-back producers disagree on sweep depth (0 ticks in `family_fail.py::_failback`, 2 ticks in `grid.py::failback_wick_c5`).
8. `label.aplus` and the absorption objects are keyed on the 6–9 box; a 6–9 H/L tag is a Jumbo object only where a Jumbo source says so, and Green Bird's A+ is the traded range (R-G11).
9. No 10:00 release calendar exists in the inventory (R-J20); no strike IV / gamma (R-R01, R-F15/F16 gamma filters); no VOLI (R-P16 uses VIX as the named substitute; VXN `[INV L610]` is the nearer twin, named); no cash NDX/SPX minutes (never invented).
10. Every projection the code draws is two-sided (`sessions.py::projections()`), but the Jumbo ladder is incomplete: no mean-reversal lines 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 and no edge → ±0.5 area, no 1.33–1.66 band as an area, no ±2, no overshoot tolerance (every touch is `t2`), no width-base variant (`w.london` only implicitly through the London box, no `w.own` ladder on the other clocks, no `w.ev`), and three predicates are one-sided by construction (`pz_edge_setup` low side, `j22_three_strike` on the path side's ±0.5, `j24_management` entry side from the path class) (R-J01, J02, J08, J09, J12, J18, J22, J23, J24, P3-01, P3-09).
11. Cross-family one-sidedness in the 2026-09-10 producers (working tree, per the family re-reads; none of it is a source reading): `f10_protected` (low only), `f13_trap_retest` and `f16_fade` (keyed on the 6–9 high), `a02_ledge_hold` / the R-A16 stand-in (the VA edge as the ledge, one side), `a08` (side chosen by the open), `s02_third_retest` (three prints at the 6–9 high, no direction), `f15_ofm` = `f18_squeeze` (alias), `f09_thinning` / `f12_arrival_aggr` (session-wide, not at the touch); each is recorded in its block's Code bullet.

### 4.4 What stays open (no invention)

- Skylit, MBP-10 depth, cash NDX/SPX minutes: not measurable; no substitute proposed.
- GEX objects the terminal draws but the text never defines: the Vol Trigger level, the volume-weighted GEX (VOL-GEX), the hedging-pressure gauge, the wall ranks beyond the first (three are drawn) — recorded on the gex page, not built; the flip computation itself is source-unspecified (three named readings).
- Tape objects tape-trusted "no" (`FINDINGS.md`): CVD, SMT, absorption B, bigtrade q90 — blocked; the rows that need them (R-F02, R-F07, R-R04, R-P18, parts of R-F06/F08/F09/S03) stay blocked.
- Unpublished Jumbo formulas: P-zone engine, EV estimator, SessionStat minimum average, the London build start, the absorption multiplier, BigTrades thresholds for other tickers — each block names its choice and never scores it as his.
- Image-only tables: XF p.11 and p.24 cells and the TBR p.22 weekday grid remain unread. The TBR p.30 charts were read from the archived page render on 2026-09-10 (the per-depth upper / lower bars at 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 / 0.5, the "Extended Range: ±0.5" console, the two average reversal times) and the earlier "TBR p.12 depth table" citation was corrected to p.30; every reading is a claim to recompute, not a source for a constant.
- Not stated by any source: an EV-width projection of the 6–9 levels (`w.ev`), the overshoot tolerance δ, the reversal definition behind the 86.46% and the p.30 bars, the mean-reversal ladder's exact depths beyond what the p.30 axis prints (0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 / 0.5). Each is a named variant or a printed column, never a faithful constant.
